# db.ts

```ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const resolvedDbPath = process.env.DATABASE_PATH
  ? path.resolve(process.cwd(), process.env.DATABASE_PATH)
  : path.join(__dirname, 'data', 'nyc_model_club.db');

fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(resolvedDbPath), { recursive: true });

export const db = new Database(resolvedDbPath);
db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

db.exec(`
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS members (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    email TEXT NOT NULL,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL,
    plan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    billing_cycle TEXT NOT NULL,
    amount_cents INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
    stripe_session_id TEXT UNIQUE,
    stripe_customer_id TEXT,
    card_last4 TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
  );

  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_members_email ON members(email);
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_members_created_at ON members(created_at DESC);

  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bookings (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    model_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    model_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    package_label TEXT NOT NULL,
    base_rate_dollars REAL NOT NULL,
    client_fee_dollars REAL NOT NULL,
    model_commission_dollars REAL NOT NULL,
    total_client_charge_dollars REAL NOT NULL,
    model_net_payout_dollars REAL NOT NULL,
    client_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    client_email TEXT NOT NULL,
    shoot_date TEXT NOT NULL,
    notes TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_payment',
    stripe_session_id TEXT UNIQUE,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
  );

  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bookings_created_at ON bookings(created_at DESC);

  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS applications (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    role TEXT NOT NULL,
    borough TEXT NOT NULL,
    email TEXT NOT NULL,
    instagram TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
  );

  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_applications_created_at ON applications(created_at DESC);

  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS newsletter_subscribers (
    email TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
  );

  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS member_onboarding (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    member_id TEXT,
    stripe_session_id TEXT UNIQUE,
    plan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    billing_cycle TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'monthly',
    email TEXT NOT NULL,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    stage_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    measurements TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    location TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    portfolio_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    digitals_url TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    studio_shoot_slot TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    company_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    company_role TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    project_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    review_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'new',
    confirmation_notified_at TEXT,
    confirmation_channel TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
  );

  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_onboarding_created_at ON member_onboarding(created_at DESC);
  CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_onboarding_status ON member_onboarding(review_status);
`);

const bookingColumns = (
  db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(bookings)').all() as Array<{ name: string }>
).map((col) => col.name);

if (!bookingColumns.includes('booking_scope')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE bookings ADD COLUMN booking_scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'model'`);
}
if (!bookingColumns.includes('production_package')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE bookings ADD COLUMN production_package TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!bookingColumns.includes('production_rate_dollars')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE bookings ADD COLUMN production_rate_dollars REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}

const memberColumns = (
  db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(members)').all() as Array<{ name: string }>
).map((col) => col.name);

if (!memberColumns.includes('webhook_verified')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN webhook_verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('date_of_birth')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN date_of_birth TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('is_minor')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN is_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('guardian_name')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN guardian_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('guardian_relationship')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN guardian_relationship TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('guardian_contact')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN guardian_contact TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('guardian_consent')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN guardian_consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('profile_visibility')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN profile_visibility TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}
if (!memberColumns.includes('visibility_scope')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN visibility_scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}

const applicationColumns = (
  db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(applications)').all() as Array<{ name: string }>
).map((col) => col.name);

if (!applicationColumns.includes('is_minor')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN is_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('guardian_name')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN guardian_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('guardian_contact')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN guardian_contact TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('guardian_consent')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN guardian_consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('profile_visibility')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN profile_visibility TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('date_of_birth')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN date_of_birth TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('guardian_relationship')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN guardian_relationship TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!applicationColumns.includes('visibility_scope')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE applications ADD COLUMN visibility_scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}

db.exec(
  `UPDATE applications
   SET visibility_scope = 'verified_clients_only',
       profile_visibility = 'clients_only'
   WHERE (is_minor = 1 OR profile_visibility = 'clients_only')
     AND (visibility_scope != 'verified_clients_only' OR profile_visibility != 'clients_only')`
);

const onboardingColumns = (
  db.prepare('PRAGMA table_info(member_onboarding)').all() as Array<{ name: string }>
).map((col) => col.name);

if (!onboardingColumns.includes('is_minor')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN is_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('guardian_name')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN guardian_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('guardian_contact')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN guardian_contact TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('guardian_consent')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN guardian_consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('profile_visibility')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN profile_visibility TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('date_of_birth')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN date_of_birth TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('guardian_relationship')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN guardian_relationship TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''`);
}
if (!onboardingColumns.includes('visibility_scope')) {
  db.exec(`ALTER TABLE member_onboarding ADD COLUMN visibility_scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public'`);
}

db.exec(
  `UPDATE member_onboarding
   SET visibility_scope = 'verified_clients_only',
       profile_visibility = 'clients_only'
   WHERE (is_minor = 1 OR profile_visibility = 'clients_only')
     AND (visibility_scope != 'verified_clients_only' OR profile_visibility != 'clients_only')`
);

export interface MemberRecord {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: string;
  plan_id: string;
  billing_cycle: string;
  amount_cents: number;
  status: string;
  stripe_session_id: string | null;
  stripe_customer_id: string | null;
  card_last4: string | null;
  webhook_verified: number;
  date_of_birth: string;
  is_minor: number;
  guardian_name: string;
  guardian_relationship: string;
  guardian_contact: string;
  guardian_consent: number;
  profile_visibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  created_at: string;
}

export interface BookingRecord {
  id: string;
  booking_scope: 'model' | 'production' | 'bundle';
  model_id: string;
  model_name: string;
  package_label: string;
  production_package: string;
  base_rate_dollars: number;
  production_rate_dollars: number;
  client_fee_dollars: number;
  model_commission_dollars: number;
  total_client_charge_dollars: number;
  model_net_payout_dollars: number;
  client_name: string;
  client_email: string;
  shoot_date: string;
  notes: string;
  status: string;
  stripe_session_id: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ApplicationRecord {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: string;
  borough: string;
  email: string;
  instagram: string;
  date_of_birth: string;
  is_minor: number;
  guardian_name: string;
  guardian_relationship: string;
  guardian_contact: string;
  guardian_consent: number;
  profile_visibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected';
  created_at: string;
}

export interface NewsletterRecord {
  email: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export type OnboardingReviewStatus = 'new' | 'scouting' | 'approved_to_roster' | 'archived';

export interface MemberOnboardingRecord {
  id: string;
  member_id: string | null;
  stripe_session_id: string | null;
  plan_id: 'talent' | 'studio' | 'clients';
  billing_cycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  stage_name: string;
  measurements: string;
  location: string;
  portfolio_url: string;
  digitals_url: string;
  studio_shoot_slot: string;
  company_name: string;
  company_role: string;
  project_type: string;
  date_of_birth: string;
  is_minor: number;
  guardian_name: string;
  guardian_relationship: string;
  guardian_contact: string;
  guardian_consent: number;
  profile_visibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  review_status: OnboardingReviewStatus;
  confirmation_notified_at: string | null;
  confirmation_channel: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export function upsertActiveMember(input: {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  fullName: string;
  role: string;
  planId: string;
  billingCycle: string;
  amountCents: number;
  stripeSessionId?: string | null;
  stripeCustomerId?: string | null;
  cardLast4?: string | null;
  webhookVerified?: boolean;
  dateOfBirth?: string;
  isMinor?: boolean;
  guardianName?: string;
  guardianRelationship?: string;
  guardianContact?: string;
  guardianConsent?: boolean;
  visibilityScope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
}): MemberRecord {
  const webhookVerifiedFlag = input.webhookVerified ? 1 : 0;
  const isMinorFlag = input.planId !== 'clients' && input.isMinor ? 1 : 0;
  const guardianConsentFlag = isMinorFlag === 1 && input.guardianConsent ? 1 : 0;
  const profileVisibility: 'public' | 'clients_only' =
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? 'clients_only' : 'public';
  const visibilityScope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only' =
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? 'verified_clients_only' : input.visibilityScope || 'public';
  const dateOfBirth = input.planId !== 'clients' ? input.dateOfBirth || '' : '';

  const existingBySession = input.stripeSessionId
    ? (db
        .prepare('SELECT * FROM members WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
        .get(input.stripeSessionId) as MemberRecord | undefined)
    : undefined;

  const existingByCustomer =
    !existingBySession && input.stripeCustomerId
      ? (db
          .prepare('SELECT * FROM members WHERE stripe_customer_id = ?')
          .get(input.stripeCustomerId) as MemberRecord | undefined)
      : undefined;

  const existing = existingBySession || existingByCustomer;
  if (existing) {
    db.prepare(
      `UPDATE members
       SET status = 'active',
           email = ?,
           full_name = ?,
           role = ?,
           plan_id = ?,
           billing_cycle = ?,
           amount_cents = ?,
           stripe_session_id = COALESCE(?, stripe_session_id),
           stripe_customer_id = COALESCE(?, stripe_customer_id),
           webhook_verified = CASE WHEN ? = 1 THEN 1 ELSE webhook_verified END,
           date_of_birth = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE date_of_birth END,
           is_minor = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE is_minor END,
           guardian_name = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE guardian_name END,
           guardian_relationship = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE guardian_relationship END,
           guardian_contact = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE guardian_contact END,
           guardian_consent = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE guardian_consent END,
           profile_visibility = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE profile_visibility END,
           visibility_scope = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE visibility_scope END
       WHERE id = ?`
    ).run(
      input.email,
      input.fullName,
      input.role,
      input.planId,
      input.billingCycle,
      input.amountCents,
      input.stripeSessionId || null,
      input.stripeCustomerId || null,
      webhookVerifiedFlag,
      dateOfBirth,
      dateOfBirth,
      dateOfBirth,
      isMinorFlag,
      dateOfBirth,
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianName || '' : '',
      dateOfBirth,
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianRelationship || '' : '',
      dateOfBirth,
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianContact || '' : '',
      dateOfBirth,
      guardianConsentFlag,
      dateOfBirth,
      profileVisibility,
      dateOfBirth,
      visibilityScope,
      existing.id
    );
    return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM members WHERE id = ?').get(existing.id) as MemberRecord;
  }

  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO members (
      id, email, full_name, role, plan_id, billing_cycle, amount_cents,
      status, stripe_session_id, stripe_customer_id, card_last4, webhook_verified,
      date_of_birth, is_minor, guardian_name, guardian_relationship, guardian_contact,
      guardian_consent, profile_visibility, visibility_scope, created_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    input.id,
    input.email,
    input.fullName,
    input.role,
    input.planId,
    input.billingCycle,
    input.amountCents,
    input.stripeSessionId || null,
    input.stripeCustomerId || null,
    input.cardLast4 || null,
    webhookVerifiedFlag,
    dateOfBirth,
    isMinorFlag,
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianName || '' : '',
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianRelationship || '' : '',
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianContact || '' : '',
    guardianConsentFlag,
    profileVisibility,
    visibilityScope,
    createdAt
  );

  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM members WHERE id = ?').get(input.id) as MemberRecord;
}

export function findMemberByStripeSession(sessionId: string): MemberRecord | undefined {
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM members WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
    .get(sessionId) as MemberRecord | undefined;
}

export function cancelMemberByStripeCustomer(customerId: string): void {
  db.prepare(`UPDATE members SET status = 'cancelled' WHERE stripe_customer_id = ?`).run(customerId);
}

export function createBookingRecord(input: {
  id: string;
  bookingScope?: 'model' | 'production' | 'bundle';
  modelId: string;
  modelName: string;
  packageLabel: string;
  productionPackage?: string;
  baseRateDollars: number;
  productionRateDollars?: number;
  clientFeeDollars: number;
  modelCommissionDollars: number;
  totalClientChargeDollars: number;
  modelNetPayoutDollars: number;
  clientName: string;
  clientEmail: string;
  shootDate: string;
  notes: string;
  status: 'pending_payment' | 'paid';
  stripeSessionId?: string | null;
}): BookingRecord {
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const bookingScope = input.bookingScope || 'model';
  const productionPackage = input.productionPackage || '';
  const productionRateDollars = input.productionRateDollars || 0;

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO bookings (
      id, booking_scope, model_id, model_name, package_label, production_package,
      base_rate_dollars, production_rate_dollars,
      client_fee_dollars, model_commission_dollars, total_client_charge_dollars,
      model_net_payout_dollars, client_name, client_email, shoot_date,
      notes, status, stripe_session_id, created_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    input.id,
    bookingScope,
    input.modelId,
    input.modelName,
    input.packageLabel,
    productionPackage,
    input.baseRateDollars,
    productionRateDollars,
    input.clientFeeDollars,
    input.modelCommissionDollars,
    input.totalClientChargeDollars,
    input.modelNetPayoutDollars,
    input.clientName,
    input.clientEmail,
    input.shootDate,
    input.notes,
    input.status,
    input.stripeSessionId || null,
    createdAt
  );

  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE id = ?').get(input.id) as BookingRecord;
}

export function markBookingPaidByStripeSession(
  sessionId: string,
  fallback?: {
    id: string;
    bookingScope?: 'model' | 'production' | 'bundle';
    modelId: string;
    modelName: string;
    packageLabel: string;
    productionPackage?: string;
    baseRateDollars: number;
    productionRateDollars?: number;
    clientFeeDollars: number;
    modelCommissionDollars: number;
    totalClientChargeDollars: number;
    modelNetPayoutDollars: number;
    clientName: string;
    clientEmail: string;
    shootDate: string;
    notes: string;
  }
): BookingRecord | undefined {
  const existing = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
    .get(sessionId) as BookingRecord | undefined;
  if (existing) {
    db.prepare(`UPDATE bookings SET status = 'paid' WHERE stripe_session_id = ?`).run(sessionId);
    return db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
      .get(sessionId) as BookingRecord;
  }

  if (fallback) {
    return createBookingRecord({
      ...fallback,
      status: 'paid',
      stripeSessionId: sessionId,
    });
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function findBookingByStripeSession(sessionId: string): BookingRecord | undefined {
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
    .get(sessionId) as BookingRecord | undefined;
}

export function createApplicationRecord(input: {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  role: string;
  borough: string;
  email: string;
  instagram: string;
  dateOfBirth?: string;
  isMinor?: boolean;
  guardianName?: string;
  guardianRelationship?: string;
  guardianContact?: string;
  guardianConsent?: boolean;
  visibilityScope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
}): ApplicationRecord {
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const isMinorFlag = input.isMinor ? 1 : 0;
  const guardianConsentFlag = input.isMinor && input.guardianConsent ? 1 : 0;
  const profileVisibility = input.isMinor ? 'clients_only' : 'public';
  const visibilityScope = input.isMinor
    ? 'verified_clients_only'
    : input.visibilityScope || 'public';

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO applications (
      id, full_name, role, borough, email, instagram,
      date_of_birth, is_minor, guardian_name, guardian_relationship, guardian_contact,
      guardian_consent, profile_visibility, visibility_scope,
      status, created_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', ?)`
  ).run(
    input.id,
    input.fullName,
    input.role,
    input.borough,
    input.email,
    input.instagram,
    input.dateOfBirth || '',
    isMinorFlag,
    input.isMinor ? input.guardianName || '' : '',
    input.isMinor ? input.guardianRelationship || '' : '',
    input.isMinor ? input.guardianContact || '' : '',
    guardianConsentFlag,
    profileVisibility,
    visibilityScope,
    createdAt
  );

  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM applications WHERE id = ?').get(input.id) as ApplicationRecord;
}

export function updateApplicationStatus(
  id: string,
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected'
): ApplicationRecord | undefined {
  const result = db.prepare('UPDATE applications SET status = ? WHERE id = ?').run(status, id);
  if (result.changes === 0) return undefined;
  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM applications WHERE id = ?').get(id) as ApplicationRecord;
}

export function addNewsletterSubscriber(email: string): NewsletterRecord {
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO newsletter_subscribers (email, created_at) VALUES (?, ?)').run(
    email,
    createdAt
  );
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM newsletter_subscribers WHERE email = ?')
    .get(email) as NewsletterRecord;
}

export function findMemberByEmailAndPlan(
  email: string,
  planId?: string
): MemberRecord | undefined {
  if (planId) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT * FROM members WHERE lower(email) = lower(?) AND plan_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`
      )
      .get(email, planId) as MemberRecord | undefined;
  }
  return db
    .prepare(`SELECT * FROM members WHERE lower(email) = lower(?) ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`)
    .get(email) as MemberRecord | undefined;
}

export function findVerifiedClientMember(
  email: string,
  memberCode: string
): MemberRecord | undefined {
  return db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT * FROM members
       WHERE lower(email) = lower(?)
         AND upper(id) = upper(?)
         AND plan_id = 'clients'
         AND status = 'active'
       ORDER BY created_at DESC
       LIMIT 1`
    )
    .get(email.trim(), memberCode.trim()) as MemberRecord | undefined;
}

export function findOnboardingByStripeSession(
  sessionId: string
): MemberOnboardingRecord | undefined {
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding WHERE stripe_session_id = ?')
    .get(sessionId) as MemberOnboardingRecord | undefined;
}

export function upsertMemberOnboarding(input: {
  id: string;
  memberId?: string | null;
  stripeSessionId?: string | null;
  planId: 'talent' | 'studio' | 'clients';
  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  email: string;
  fullName: string;
  stageName: string;
  measurements: string;
  location: string;
  portfolioUrl: string;
  digitalsUrl: string;
  studioShootSlot: string;
  companyName: string;
  companyRole: string;
  projectType: string;
  dateOfBirth?: string;
  isMinor?: boolean;
  guardianName?: string;
  guardianRelationship?: string;
  guardianContact?: string;
  guardianConsent?: boolean;
  visibilityScope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  confirmationNotifiedAt?: string | null;
  confirmationChannel?: string;
}): MemberOnboardingRecord {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const isMinorFlag = input.planId !== 'clients' && input.isMinor ? 1 : 0;
  const guardianConsentFlag = isMinorFlag === 1 && input.guardianConsent ? 1 : 0;
  const profileVisibility = isMinorFlag === 1 ? 'clients_only' : 'public';
  const visibilityScope =
    isMinorFlag === 1
      ? 'verified_clients_only'
      : input.visibilityScope || 'public';
  const dateOfBirth = input.planId !== 'clients' ? input.dateOfBirth || '' : '';
  const existing = input.stripeSessionId
    ? findOnboardingByStripeSession(input.stripeSessionId)
    : undefined;

  if (existing) {
    db.prepare(
      `UPDATE member_onboarding
       SET member_id = COALESCE(?, member_id),
           plan_id = ?,
           billing_cycle = ?,
           email = ?,
           full_name = ?,
           stage_name = ?,
           measurements = ?,
           location = ?,
           portfolio_url = ?,
           digitals_url = ?,
           studio_shoot_slot = ?,
           company_name = ?,
           company_role = ?,
           project_type = ?,
           date_of_birth = ?,
           is_minor = ?,
           guardian_name = ?,
           guardian_relationship = ?,
           guardian_contact = ?,
           guardian_consent = ?,
           profile_visibility = ?,
           visibility_scope = ?,
           confirmation_notified_at = COALESCE(?, confirmation_notified_at),
           confirmation_channel = CASE WHEN ? != '' THEN ? ELSE confirmation_channel END,
           updated_at = ?
       WHERE id = ?`
    ).run(
      input.memberId || null,
      input.planId,
      input.billingCycle,
      input.email,
      input.fullName,
      input.stageName,
      input.measurements,
      input.location,
      input.portfolioUrl,
      input.digitalsUrl,
      input.studioShootSlot,
      input.companyName,
      input.companyRole,
      input.projectType,
      dateOfBirth,
      isMinorFlag,
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianName || '' : '',
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianRelationship || '' : '',
      isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianContact || '' : '',
      guardianConsentFlag,
      profileVisibility,
      visibilityScope,
      input.confirmationNotifiedAt || null,
      input.confirmationChannel || '',
      input.confirmationChannel || '',
      now,
      existing.id
    );
    return db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding WHERE id = ?')
      .get(existing.id) as MemberOnboardingRecord;
  }

  db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO member_onboarding (
      id, member_id, stripe_session_id, plan_id, billing_cycle, email,
      full_name, stage_name, measurements, location, portfolio_url, digitals_url,
      studio_shoot_slot, company_name, company_role, project_type,
      date_of_birth, is_minor, guardian_name, guardian_relationship, guardian_contact,
      guardian_consent, profile_visibility, visibility_scope,
      review_status, confirmation_notified_at, confirmation_channel, created_at, updated_at
    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'new', ?, ?, ?, ?)`
  ).run(
    input.id,
    input.memberId || null,
    input.stripeSessionId || null,
    input.planId,
    input.billingCycle,
    input.email,
    input.fullName,
    input.stageName,
    input.measurements,
    input.location,
    input.portfolioUrl,
    input.digitalsUrl,
    input.studioShootSlot,
    input.companyName,
    input.companyRole,
    input.projectType,
    dateOfBirth,
    isMinorFlag,
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianName || '' : '',
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianRelationship || '' : '',
    isMinorFlag === 1 ? input.guardianContact || '' : '',
    guardianConsentFlag,
    profileVisibility,
    visibilityScope,
    input.confirmationNotifiedAt || now,
    input.confirmationChannel || 'in_app_dispatch',
    now,
    now
  );

  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding WHERE id = ?')
    .get(input.id) as MemberOnboardingRecord;
}

export function updateOnboardingNotification(
  id: string,
  channel: string,
  notifiedAt: string
): MemberOnboardingRecord | undefined {
  db.prepare(
    `UPDATE member_onboarding SET confirmation_channel = ?, confirmation_notified_at = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`
  ).run(channel, notifiedAt, new Date().toISOString(), id);
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding WHERE id = ?')
    .get(id) as MemberOnboardingRecord | undefined;
}

export function updateOnboardingReviewStatus(
  id: string,
  reviewStatus: OnboardingReviewStatus
): MemberOnboardingRecord | undefined {
  const result = db
    .prepare('UPDATE member_onboarding SET review_status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?')
    .run(reviewStatus, new Date().toISOString(), id);
  if (result.changes === 0) return undefined;
  return db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding WHERE id = ?')
    .get(id) as MemberOnboardingRecord;
}

export function listApprovedOnboardingTalent(options?: { includeClientsOnly?: boolean }): Array<{
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  stageName: string;
  measurements: string;
  location: string;
  portfolioUrl: string;
  digitalsUrl: string;
  planId: string;
  isMinor: boolean;
  profileVisibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibilityScope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  createdAt: string;
}> {
  const includeClientsOnly = Boolean(options?.includeClientsOnly);
  const query = includeClientsOnly
    ? `SELECT id, full_name, stage_name, measurements, location, portfolio_url, digitals_url, plan_id, is_minor, profile_visibility, visibility_scope, created_at
       FROM member_onboarding
       WHERE review_status = 'approved_to_roster' AND plan_id IN ('talent', 'studio')
       ORDER BY updated_at DESC`
    : `SELECT id, full_name, stage_name, measurements, location, portfolio_url, digitals_url, plan_id, is_minor, profile_visibility, visibility_scope, created_at
       FROM member_onboarding
       WHERE review_status = 'approved_to_roster'
         AND plan_id IN ('talent', 'studio')
         AND is_minor = 0
         AND profile_visibility = 'public'
         AND visibility_scope = 'public'
       ORDER BY updated_at DESC`;

  const rows = db.prepare(query).all() as Array<{
    id: string;
    full_name: string;
    stage_name: string;
    measurements: string;
    location: string;
    portfolio_url: string;
    digitals_url: string;
    plan_id: string;
    is_minor: number;
    profile_visibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
    visibility_scope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
    created_at: string;
  }>;

  return rows.map((row) => ({
    id: row.id,
    fullName: row.full_name,
    stageName: row.stage_name,
    measurements: row.measurements,
    location: row.location,
    portfolioUrl: row.portfolio_url,
    digitalsUrl: row.digitals_url,
    planId: row.plan_id,
    isMinor: row.is_minor === 1,
    profileVisibility: row.is_minor === 1 ? 'clients_only' : row.profile_visibility || 'public',
    visibilityScope:
      row.is_minor === 1 || row.profile_visibility === 'clients_only'
        ? 'verified_clients_only'
        : row.visibility_scope || 'public',
    createdAt: row.created_at,
  }));
}

export function listApprovedDirectoryApplications(options?: {
  includeClientsOnly?: boolean;
}): Array<{
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  role: string;
  borough: string;
  isMinor: boolean;
  profileVisibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibilityScope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  createdAt: string;
}> {
  const includeClientsOnly = Boolean(options?.includeClientsOnly);
  const query = includeClientsOnly
    ? `SELECT id, full_name, role, borough, is_minor, profile_visibility, visibility_scope, created_at
       FROM applications
       WHERE status = 'approved'
       ORDER BY created_at DESC`
    : `SELECT id, full_name, role, borough, is_minor, profile_visibility, visibility_scope, created_at
       FROM applications
       WHERE status = 'approved'
         AND is_minor = 0
         AND profile_visibility = 'public'
         AND visibility_scope = 'public'
       ORDER BY created_at DESC`;

  const rows = db.prepare(query).all() as Array<{
    id: string;
    full_name: string;
    role: string;
    borough: string;
    is_minor: number;
    profile_visibility: 'public' | 'clients_only';
    visibility_scope: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
    created_at: string;
  }>;

  return rows.map((row) => ({
    id: row.id,
    fullName: row.full_name,
    role: row.role,
    borough: row.borough,
    isMinor: row.is_minor === 1,
    profileVisibility: row.is_minor === 1 ? 'clients_only' : row.profile_visibility || 'public',
    visibilityScope:
      row.is_minor === 1 || row.profile_visibility === 'clients_only'
        ? 'verified_clients_only'
        : row.visibility_scope || 'public',
    createdAt: row.created_at,
  }));
}

export function getAdminDashboardData() {
  const members = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM members ORDER BY created_at DESC')
    .all() as MemberRecord[];
  const bookings = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM bookings ORDER BY created_at DESC')
    .all() as BookingRecord[];
  const applications = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM applications ORDER BY created_at DESC')
    .all() as ApplicationRecord[];
  const newsletterSubscribers = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM newsletter_subscribers ORDER BY created_at DESC')
    .all() as NewsletterRecord[];
  const onboarding = db
    .prepare('SELECT * FROM member_onboarding ORDER BY created_at DESC')
    .all() as MemberOnboardingRecord[];

  return {
    members,
    bookings,
    applications,
    newsletterSubscribers,
    onboarding,
    stats: {
      activeMembersCount: members.filter((m) => m.status === 'active').length,
      totalBookingsCount: bookings.length,
      paidBookingsCount: bookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'paid').length,
      pendingApplicationsCount: applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'pending').length,
      newsletterCount: newsletterSubscribers.length,
      newOnboardingCount: onboarding.filter((o) => o.review_status === 'new').length,
      totalOnboardingCount: onboarding.length,
    },
  };
}

# Run and deploy your AI Studio app

This contains everything you need to run your app locally.

View your app in AI Studio: https://ai.studio/apps/775a4bf4-09a5-4673-bf82-196176cbaae5

## Run Locally

**Prerequisites:**  Node.js


1. Install dependencies:
   `npm install`
2. Set the `GEMINI_API_KEY` in [.env.local](.env.local) to your Gemini API key
3. Run the app:
   `npm run dev`

```

import express from 'express';

  findMemberByStripeSession,
  findOnboardingByStripeSession,
  findVerifiedClientMember,
  getAdminDashboardData,
  listApprovedDirectoryApplications,
  listApprovedOnboardingTalent,

  updateApplicationStatus,
  updateOnboardingNotification,
  updateOnboardingReviewStatus,
  upsertActiveMember,
  upsertMemberOnboarding,
} from './db.ts';

dotenv.config();

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

function resolveServerPort(): number {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    const arg = args[i];
    if (arg === '--port' && args[i + 1]) {
      const parsed = Number(args[i + 1]);
      if (Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0) return parsed;
    } else if (arg.startsWith('--port=')) {
      const parsed = Number(arg.slice('--port='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0) return parsed;
    }
  }

  const envPort = Number(process.env.PORT);
  const nginxPort = Number(process.env.NGINX_PORT);
  if (!isProd || (Number.isFinite(nginxPort) && nginxPort > 0 && envPort === nginxPort)) {
    return Number(process.env.DEFAULT_APP_PORT) || 3000;
  }

  return Number.isFinite(envPort) && envPort > 0 ? envPort : 3000;
}

const PORT = resolveServerPort();

type StripeKeyMode = 'stripe_live' | 'stripe_test' | 'test_fallback' | 'disabled';

function isPlaceholderStripeValue(val: string): boolean {
  if (!val) return true;
  const upper = val.toUpperCase();
  return (
    upper.includes('YOUR_') ||
    upper.includes('PLACEHOLDER') ||
    upper.includes('REPLACE_ME') ||
    upper.includes('CHANGEME') ||
    val.includes('<') ||
    val.includes('>')
  );
}

function getStripeConfig(): {
  secretKey: string;
  publishableKey: string;
  webhookSecret: string;
  testFallbackAllowed: boolean;
  mode: StripeKeyMode;
} {
  const rawSecret = (process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY || '').trim();
  const rawPublishable = (
    process.env.STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
    process.env.VITE_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ||
    ''
  ).trim();
  const rawWebhook = (process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET || '').trim();

  const isRealSecretFormat =
    rawSecret.startsWith('sk_test_') ||
    rawSecret.startsWith('sk_live_') ||
    rawSecret.startsWith('rk_test_') ||
    rawSecret.startsWith('rk_live_');
  const secretKey = !isPlaceholderStripeValue(rawSecret) && isRealSecretFormat ? rawSecret : '';

  const isRealPublishableFormat =
    rawPublishable.startsWith('pk_test_') || rawPublishable.startsWith('pk_live_');
  const publishableKey =
    !isPlaceholderStripeValue(rawPublishable) && isRealPublishableFormat ? rawPublishable : '';

  const webhookSecret = !isPlaceholderStripeValue(rawWebhook) ? rawWebhook : '';
  const testFallbackAllowed = process.env.STRIPE_TEST_MODE?.trim().toLowerCase() !== 'false';

  let mode: StripeKeyMode = 'disabled';
  if (secretKey.startsWith('sk_live_') || secretKey.startsWith('rk_live_')) {
    mode = 'stripe_live';
  } else if (secretKey.startsWith('sk_test_') || secretKey.startsWith('rk_test_')) {
    mode = 'stripe_test';
  } else if (testFallbackAllowed) {
    mode = 'test_fallback';
  }

  return {
    secretKey,
    publishableKey,
    webhookSecret,
    testFallbackAllowed,
    mode,
  };
}

function getStripeClient(): Stripe | null {
  const { secretKey } = getStripeConfig();
  if (!secretKey) {
    return null;
  }
  return new Stripe(secretKey);
}

function getWebhookSecret(): string {
  return getStripeConfig().webhookSecret;
}

function isStrictWebhookMode(): boolean {
  return Boolean(getStripeClient() && getWebhookSecret());
}

type SquareKeyMode = 'square_live' | 'square_sandbox' | 'test_fallback' | 'disabled';

function getSquareConfig(): {
  accessToken: string;
  locationId: string;
  env: 'production' | 'sandbox';
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  testFallbackAllowed: boolean;
  mode: SquareKeyMode;
} {
  const rawToken = (process.env.SQUARE_ACCESS_TOKEN || '').trim();
  const rawLocation = (process.env.SQUARE_LOCATION_ID || '').trim();
  const rawEnv = (process.env.SQUARE_ENV || 'sandbox').trim().toLowerCase();
  const env: 'production' | 'sandbox' = rawEnv === 'production' ? 'production' : 'sandbox';

  const accessToken =
    rawToken && !isPlaceholderStripeValue(rawToken) ? rawToken : '';
  const locationId =
    rawLocation && !isPlaceholderStripeValue(rawLocation) ? rawLocation : '';
  const testFallbackAllowed =
    process.env.SQUARE_TEST_MODE?.trim().toLowerCase() !== 'false';

  let mode: SquareKeyMode = 'disabled';
  if (accessToken && locationId) {
    mode = env === 'production' ? 'square_live' : 'square_sandbox';
  } else if (testFallbackAllowed) {
    mode = 'test_fallback';
  }

  return {
    accessToken,
    locationId,
    env,
    apiBaseUrl:
      env === 'production'
        ? 'https://connect.squareup.com'
        : 'https://connect.squareupsandbox.com',
    testFallbackAllowed,
    mode,
  };
}

function isSquareSessionId(sessionId: string): boolean {
  return sessionId.startsWith('sq_');
}

async function createSquarePaymentLink(params: {
  name: string;
  amountCents: number;
  redirectUrl: string;
  referenceId: string;
}): Promise<{ url: string; orderId: string; linkId: string }> {
  const cfg = getSquareConfig();
  if (!cfg.accessToken || !cfg.locationId) {
    throw new Error('Square credentials are not configured.');
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${cfg.apiBaseUrl}/v2/online-checkout/payment-links`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${cfg.accessToken}`,
      'Square-Version': '2024-11-20',
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      idempotency_key: crypto.randomUUID(),
      quick_pay: {
        name: params.name.slice(0, 250),
        price_money: {
          amount: Math.max(100, Math.round(params.amountCents)),
          currency: 'USD',
        },
        location_id: cfg.locationId,
      },
      checkout_options: {
        redirect_url: params.redirectUrl,
      },
      payment_note: params.referenceId.slice(0, 500),
    }),
  });

  const payload = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
    payment_link?: { id?: string; url?: string; order_id?: string };
    errors?: Array<{ detail?: string; code?: string }>;
  };

  if (!response.ok || !payload.payment_link?.url) {
    const errDetail =
      payload.errors?.[0]?.detail ||
      payload.errors?.[0]?.code ||
      `Square API returned status ${response.status}.`;
    throw new Error(errDetail);
  }

  return {
    url: payload.payment_link.url,
    orderId: payload.payment_link.order_id || '',
    linkId: payload.payment_link.id || '',
  };
}

async function retrieveSquareOrderState(orderId: string): Promise<{
  paid: boolean;
  state: string;
  totalCents: number;
}> {
  const cfg = getSquareConfig();
  if (!cfg.accessToken || !orderId) {
    return { paid: false, state: 'UNKNOWN', totalCents: 0 };
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${cfg.apiBaseUrl}/v2/orders/${encodeURIComponent(orderId)}`, {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${cfg.accessToken}`,
      'Square-Version': '2024-11-20',
    },
  });

  const payload = (await response.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
    order?: {
      state?: string;
      total_money?: { amount?: number };
      tenders?: Array<{ card_details?: { status?: string } }>;
    };
  };

  const state = payload.order?.state || 'UNKNOWN';
  const hasCapturedTender = Boolean(
    payload.order?.tenders?.some((t) => t.card_details?.status === 'CAPTURED')
  );
  const paid = state === 'COMPLETED' || hasCapturedTender;

  return {
    paid,
    state,
    totalCents: Number(payload.order?.total_money?.amount || 0),
  };
}
