```tsx
          const resolvedLabel = resolvedProvider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe';
          if (data?.confirmed) {
            setVerifiedBooking({
              reference: data.reference || sessionId.slice(-6).toUpperCase(),
              modelName: data.modelName || fallbackModel,
              provider: resolvedProvider,
            });
          } else {
            setBookingNotice(
              `Your ${resolvedLabel} booking payment is processing. Refresh shortly to confirm status.`
            );
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    } else if (bookingParam === 'cancelled') {
      setBookingNotice(
        'Online booking checkout was cancelled. Select any represented model below to resume.'
      );
    }
  }, []);

  const navigateTo = (pathWithQuery: string) => {
    const parsed = new URL(pathWithQuery, window.location.origin);
    if (window.location.pathname + window.location.search !== parsed.pathname + parsed.search) {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', parsed.pathname + parsed.search);
      setCurrentPath(parsed.pathname);
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'auto' });
    }
  };

  const handleNavigateFromOnboarding = (target?: {
    sectionId?: string;
    workCategory?: 'Digitals';
    clientUnlocked?: boolean;
  }) => {
    if (target?.clientUnlocked) {
      sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_unlocked', '1');
      const sid = sessionStorage.getItem('nycmc_last_session_id');
      if (sid) {
        sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_session_id', sid);
      }
      setClientAccessUnlocked(true);
      refreshApprovedTalentAndDirectory(sid);
    }
    navigateTo('/');
    if (target?.sectionId) {
      const targetId = target.sectionId;
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        const el = document.getElementById(targetId);
        if (el) {
          el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      }, 80);
    }
  };

  const defaultTalentTier =
    MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.find((t) => t.id === 'talent') || MEMBERSHIP_TIERS[MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.length - 1];

  const scrollToSection = (id: string) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  const handleStartStripeCheckout = async (
    tier: MembershipTier,
    billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual',
    providerOverride?: PaymentProvider
  ) => {
    const activeProvider = providerOverride || paymentProvider;
    const providerLabel = activeProvider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe';
    setPaymentProvider(activeProvider);
    setCheckoutError(null);
    setErrorTierId(null);
    setCheckoutNotice(null);
    setLoadingTierId(tier.id);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/create-checkout-session', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          planId: tier.id,
          billingCycle,
          paymentProvider: activeProvider,
        }),
      });
      const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        mode?: string;
        url?: string | null;
        sessionId?: string | null;
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok) {
        setErrorTierId(tier.id);
        setCheckoutError(
          data.error || `Unable to start ${providerLabel} checkout (status ${res.status}).`
        );
        return;
      }

      if (data.mode === 'stripe_redirect' || data.mode === 'square_redirect') {
        if (!data.url) {
          setErrorTierId(tier.id);
          setCheckoutError(`${providerLabel} checkout did not return a redirect URL.`);
          return;
        }
        window.location.assign(data.url);
        return;
      }

      if (data.mode === 'stripe_embedded' || data.mode === 'square_embedded') {
        setCheckoutSessionId(data.sessionId || null);
        setCheckoutCycle(billingCycle);
        setCheckoutTier(tier);
        return;
      }

      setErrorTierId(tier.id);
      setCheckoutError('Unexpected checkout response from server.');
    } catch {
      setErrorTierId(tier.id);
      setCheckoutError(`Network error initializing ${providerLabel} Checkout.`);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTierId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleNewsletterSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setNewsletterStatus('submitting');
    setNewsletterMessage('');

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/newsletter', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: newsletterEmail }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setNewsletterStatus('error');
        setNewsletterMessage(data.error || 'Please enter a valid email address.');
        return;
      }
      setNewsletterStatus('done');
      setNewsletterMessage(`Subscribed ${data.email} to NYC Model Club dispatches.`);
      setNewsletterEmail('');
    } catch {
      setNewsletterStatus('error');
      setNewsletterMessage('Unable to subscribe right now.');
    }
  };

  const handleVerifyClientLogin = async (
    email: string,
    memberCode: string
  ): Promise<string | null> => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/client-access/verify', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: email.trim(), memberCode: memberCode.trim() }),
      });
      const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        verified?: boolean;
        sessionId?: string;
        memberCode?: string;
        planName?: string;
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok || !data.verified || !data.sessionId) {
        return data.error || 'Unable to verify Clients & Agencies pass.';
      }
      sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_unlocked', '1');
      sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_session_id', data.sessionId);
      sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_last_session_id', data.sessionId);
      setClientAccessUnlocked(true);
      setActiveMemberBadge({
        planName: data.planName || 'NYC Model Club: Clients & Agencies Access',
        memberCode: data.memberCode || memberCode.trim().toUpperCase(),
      });
      refreshApprovedTalentAndDirectory(data.sessionId);
      return null;
    } catch {
      return 'Network error verifying Clients & Agencies credentials.';
    }
  };

  const handleLogoutClientAccess = () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem('nycmc_client_unlocked');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('nycmc_client_session_id');
    setClientAccessUnlocked(false);
    refreshApprovedTalentAndDirectory(null);
  };

  const allDirectoryMembers = [...approvedDirectoryMembers, ...DIRECTORY_MEMBERS].filter(
    (m) =>
      clientAccessUnlocked ||
      (!m.isMinor &&
        m.visibility !== 'clients_only' &&
        m.visibilityScope !== 'verified_clients_only')
  );

  const modelDirectory = allDirectoryMembers.filter(
    (m) =>
      m.category === 'model' &&
      (m.name.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        m.role.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()))
  );

  const creativeDirectory = allDirectoryMembers.filter(
    (m) =>
      m.category === 'creative' &&
      (m.name.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()) ||
        m.role.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()))
  );

  if (currentPath === '/admin' || currentPath === '/admin/') {
    return <AdminDashboard onNavigateHome={() => navigateTo('/')} />;
  }

  if (
    currentPath === '/onboarding' ||
    currentPath === '/onboarding/' ||
    currentPath === '/welcome' ||
    currentPath === '/welcome/'
  ) {
    return (
      <OnboardingPage
        onNavigateHome={handleNavigateFromOnboarding}
        onStartMembershipCheckout={(planId: MembershipPlanId) => {
          const tier = MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.find((t) => t.id === planId) || defaultTalentTier;
          navigateTo('/');
          void handleStartStripeCheckout(tier, 'monthly');
        }}
        onVerifiedMemberBadge={(badge) => {
          setActiveMemberBadge({
            planName: badge.planName,
            memberCode: badge.memberCode,
          });
          if (badge.planId === 'clients') {
            sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_unlocked', '1');
            setClientAccessUnlocked(true);
          }
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-white text-black flex flex-col">
      {/* Top Bar Contract: 3 zones separated by gap-8 */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-xs border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 h-14 flex items-center justify-between gap-8">
          {/* Zone 1: Single-line brand wordmark */}
          <a
            href="#top"
            className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-[-0.04em] text-black whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            nyc model club.
          </a>

          {/* Zone 2: 5 concise single-line nav links */}
          <nav
            aria-label="Primary navigation"
            className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-6 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700"
          >
            <a
              href="#directory"
              className="hover:text-black hover:underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Directory
            </a>
            <a
              href="#membership"
              className="hover:text-black hover:underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Membership
            </a>
            <a
              href="#media-production"
              className="hover:text-black hover:underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Media Works
            </a>
            <a
              href="#management"
              className="hover:text-black hover:underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Models &amp; AI
            </a>
            <a
              href="#management"
              className="hover:text-black hover:underline underline-offset-4 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 font-semibold text-black"
            >
              NYC Model Management
            </a>
          </nav>

          {/* Zone 3: 1 primary action */}
          <div className="flex items-center shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleStartStripeCheckout(defaultTalentTier, 'monthly')}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              Join Club
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main id="top" className="flex-1">
        {/* SECTION 1: Editorial Hero Cover Story */}
        <section className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 pt-4 pb-10 border-b border-neutral-200">
          <div className="relative aspect-[16/9] md:aspect-[21/9] w-full overflow-hidden border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100">
            {heroSlide === 0 && !heroImgFailed ? (
              <>
                <img
                  src="/assets/aistudio/image_be9b51bd.png"
                  alt="NYC Model Club, Hudson Yards Rooftop Editorial Shoot"
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  onError={() => setHeroImgFailed(true)}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover object-[50%_35%]"
                />
                <div className="absolute bottom-2.5 left-3 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase text-black/70 bg-white/75 px-1.5 py-0.5 pointer-events-none">
                  NYC MODEL CLUB
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <EditorialPlate
                variant={
                  heroSlide === 0 ? 'hero_editorial' : heroSlide === 1 ? 'studio_group' : 'campaign_warm'
                }
                className="w-full h-full transition-opacity duration-300"
                label="NYC Model Club, Editorial Cover"
              />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 text-center max-w-6xl mx-auto">
            {heroSlide !== 0 && (
              <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 mb-2">
                {heroSlide === 1 ? 'Direct Talent Booking' : 'SoHo Studio & Catalogs'}
              </p>
            )}
            <h1
              className="font-display text-black leading-[1.1] lg:whitespace-nowrap text-balance"
              style={{ fontSize: 'clamp(1.45rem, 3.05vw, 2.7rem)' }}
            >
              {heroSlide === 0 ? (
                <>
                  <span className="font-bold tracking-[-0.03em]">NYC Model Club</span>{' '}
                  <span className="font-light tracking-[0.015em] text-neutral-700">
                    &middot; Open to All Models
                  </span>
                </>
              ) : heroSlide === 1 ? (
                <>
                  <span className="font-bold tracking-[-0.03em]">NYC Model Club</span>{' '}
                  <span className="font-light tracking-[0.015em] text-neutral-700">
                    &middot; Book Represented &amp; AI Talent
                  </span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <span className="font-bold tracking-[-0.03em]">SoHo Production</span>{' '}
                  <span className="font-light tracking-[0.015em] text-neutral-700">
                    &middot; Media &amp; Studio Access
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-3 text-base md:text-lg text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl mx-auto">
              {heroSlide === 0
                ? '“Stars do not pull each other down to be more visible; they shine brighter.”'
                : heroSlide === 1
                  ? 'Book curated runway, editorial, commercial, and AI models directly through our agency desk, with preferred rates for Clients & Agencies members.'
                  : 'Turnkey Crosby Street photography, film & motion, and lookbook production, available standalone or bundled with represented talent.'}
            </p>

            {/* Pagination dots */}
            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2" role="tablist" aria-label="Hero stories">
              {[0, 1, 2].map((idx) => (
                <button
                  key={idx}
                  type="button"
                  role="tab"
                  aria-selected={heroSlide === idx}
                  aria-label={`Hero slide ${idx + 1}`}
                  onClick={() => setHeroSlide(idx)}
                  className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all ${
                    heroSlide === idx ? 'w-5 bg-black' : 'w-1.5 bg-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-500'
                  }`}
                />
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('membership')}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                Join Membership
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('management')}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 border border-black text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                Represented Talent
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SECTION 1B: Model-Facing Value Proposition Strip (3 Core Pillars) */}
        <section
          aria-label="Model platform pillars"
          className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50"
        >
          <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 divide-y md:divide-y-0 md:divide-x divide-neutral-200">
            {MODEL_VALUE_PILLARS.map((pillar, idx) => (
              <div
                key={pillar.indexLabel}
                className={`py-6 ${
                  idx === 0
                    ? 'md:pr-8'
                    : idx === 1
                      ? 'md:px-8'
                      : 'md:pl-8'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-neutral-500">
                  {pillar.indexLabel} · {pillar.categoryTag}
                </span>
                <h2 className="mt-1.5 font-display font-bold text-lg tracking-tight text-black">
                  {pillar.title}
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs md:text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                  {pillar.description}
                </p>
                {idx === 2 && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setInfoModal('contact')}
                    className="mt-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-neutral-600 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Safety &amp; Support Hub
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SECTION 2: Pro News / Club Intelligence (Single Compact Row) */}
        <section className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-10">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2.5 mb-5">
            <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight text-black">
              Pro News.
            </h2>
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Casting &amp; Industry Intelligence
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {PRO_NEWS_STORIES.slice(0, 3).map((story) => (
              <button
                key={story.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedStory(story)}
                className="group text-left flex flex-col focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-black"
              >
                <div className="aspect-[4/3] w-full overflow-hidden border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100">
                  {story.imageSrc && !failedStoryImages[story.id] ? (
                    <img
                      src={story.imageSrc}
                      alt={story.title}
                      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                      onError={() =>
                        setFailedStoryImages((prev) => ({ ...prev, [story.id]: true }))
                      }
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <EditorialPlate
                      variant={story.variant}
                      className="w-full h-full transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                      label={story.title}
                    />
                  )}
                </div>
                <span className="mt-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
                  {story.kicker}
                </span>
                <h3 className="mt-1 font-display font-semibold text-sm leading-snug text-black group-hover:underline underline-offset-4">
                  {story.title}
                </h3>
                <span className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
                  {story.byline}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SECTION 6: NYC Model Club Membership Fee (Stripe & Square) */}
        <MembershipSection
          onSelectStripePlan={handleStartStripeCheckout}
          paymentProvider={paymentProvider}
          onChangePaymentProvider={setPaymentProvider}
          activeMemberBadge={activeMemberBadge}
          loadingTierId={loadingTierId}
          checkoutNotice={checkoutNotice}
          checkoutError={checkoutError}
          errorTierId={errorTierId}
          onOpenOnboarding={() => {
            const savedSid = sessionStorage.getItem('nycmc_last_session_id') || '';
            navigateTo(savedSid ? `/onboarding?session_id=${encodeURIComponent(savedSid)}` : '/onboarding');
          }}
        />

        {/* SECTION 7: LAYER 2 — New York City Model Management (Models & AI Talent Division) */}
        <ManagementSection
          onApplyMembership={() => scrollToSection('membership')}
          stripeConfigured={serverHealth.stripeConfigured}
          squareConfigured={serverHealth.squareConfigured}
          paymentProvider={paymentProvider}
          onChangePaymentProvider={setPaymentProvider}
          isProd={serverHealth.isProd}
          verifiedBooking={verifiedBooking}
          bookingNotice={bookingNotice}
          approvedRosterModels={approvedRosterModels}
          clientAccessUnlocked={clientAccessUnlocked}
          onVerifyClientLogin={handleVerifyClientLogin}
          onLogoutClientAccess={handleLogoutClientAccess}
        />

        {/* SECTION 7B: DIVISION 02 — Media & Visual Works (Photography, Film, Visuals & Catalogs) */}
        <MediaProductionSection
          stripeConfigured={serverHealth.stripeConfigured}
          squareConfigured={serverHealth.squareConfigured}
          paymentProvider={paymentProvider}
          onChangePaymentProvider={setPaymentProvider}
          isProd={serverHealth.isProd}
          clientAccessUnlocked={clientAccessUnlocked}
          onUnlockClientAccess={() => scrollToSection('membership')}
        />

        {/* SECTION 8: Club Directory (Compact Searchable Roster & Creatives) */}
        <section id="directory" className="bg-white text-black py-12 md:py-14 border-t border-b border-neutral-200">
          <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-neutral-200 mb-8">
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl md:text-4xl font-normal leading-tight text-black">
                  Member Directory.
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                  Active models and creative collaborators across Manhattan &amp; Brooklyn
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 border border-neutral-300 w-full sm:w-64">
                <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={directorySearch}
                  onChange={(e) => setDirectorySearch(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Filter models or creatives..."
                  aria-label="Filter directory"
                  className="w-full text-xs text-black placeholder:text-neutral-400 focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {(() => {
              const hasSearchQuery = directorySearch.trim().length > 0;
              const resolvedTab: 'models' | 'creatives' | null = hasSearchQuery
                ? activeDirectoryTab === 'creatives' && creativeDirectory.length > 0
                  ? 'creatives'
                  : modelDirectory.length > 0
                    ? 'models'
                    : creativeDirectory.length > 0
                      ? 'creatives'
                      : null
                : activeDirectoryTab;

              const visibleMembers =
                resolvedTab === 'models'
                  ? modelDirectory.slice(0, 3)
                  : resolvedTab === 'creatives'
                    ? creativeDirectory.slice(0, 3)
                    : [];

              const actionLabel =
                resolvedTab === 'creatives' ? 'SUBMIT PROFILE' : 'APPLY TO DIRECTORY';

              return (
                <div>
                  {/* Unified Tab Header Bar */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-4 py-3">
                    <div
                      className="inline-flex border border-neutral-300 bg-white"
                      role="tablist"
                      aria-label="Member directory categories"
                    >
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        role="tab"
                        aria-selected={resolvedTab === 'models'}
                        onClick={() =>
                          setActiveDirectoryTab((prev) =>
                            prev === 'models' && !hasSearchQuery ? null : 'models'
                          )
                        }
                        className={`px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                          resolvedTab === 'models'
                            ? 'bg-black text-white'
                            : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Models
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        role="tab"
                        aria-selected={resolvedTab === 'creatives'}
                        onClick={() =>
                          setActiveDirectoryTab((prev) =>
                            prev === 'creatives' && !hasSearchQuery ? null : 'creatives'
                          )
                        }
                        className={`px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide border-l border-neutral-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                          resolvedTab === 'creatives'
                            ? 'bg-black text-white'
                            : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-100'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Creatives
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setJoinModalOpen(true)}
                      className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider underline underline-offset-4 text-neutral-600 hover:text-black cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                    >
                      {actionLabel}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  {/* Full-width Expanded List Directly Underneath */}
                  {resolvedTab && visibleMembers.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="border-x border-b border-neutral-200 bg-white grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 divide-y sm:divide-y-0 sm:divide-x divide-neutral-200">
                      {visibleMembers.map((m) => (
                        <button
                          key={m.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setJoinModalOpen(true)}
                          className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 text-left px-5 py-4 hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-300 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-black">
                            {m.initials}
                          </div>
                          <div className="min-w-0">
                            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-black truncate">{m.name}</p>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{m.role}</p>
                            {(m.isMinor ||
                              m.visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                              m.visibilityScope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                              <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 truncate">
                                Private Minor · Clients Only
                              </p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {hasSearchQuery && !resolvedTab && (
                    <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                      No matching models or creatives found for &ldquo;{directorySearch.trim()}&rdquo;.
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })()}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SECTION 9: Latest Work (Single Clean Row) */}
        <section id="latest-work" className="bg-white text-black py-12 md:py-14">
          <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
            <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-4 mb-7">
              <h2 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl md:text-4xl font-normal leading-tight text-black">
                Latest Work.
              </h2>

              <span className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-medium tracking-wide whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                All Latest
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-5">
              {LATEST_WORK.slice(0, 5).map((item) => {
                const workImgKey = `work-${item.id}`;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setLightboxFrame({
                        id: item.id,
                        plateVariant: item.variant,
                        imageSrc: item.imageSrc,
                        title: item.brand,
                        caption: item.subtitle,
                        credit: item.category,
                      })
                    }
                    className="group text-left focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-black cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <div className="aspect-[3/4] w-full overflow-hidden border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100">
                      {item.imageSrc && !failedStoryImages[workImgKey] ? (
                        <img
                          src={item.imageSrc}
                          alt={item.brand}
                          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                          onError={() =>
                            setFailedStoryImages((prev) => ({ ...prev, [workImgKey]: true }))
                          }
                          className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <EditorialPlate
                          variant={item.variant}
                          className="w-full h-full transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                          label={item.brand}
                        />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-bold text-black">{item.brand}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{item.category}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 truncate">{item.subtitle}</p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      {/* SECTION 10: Dark Editorial Footer */}
      <footer className="bg-[#141414] text-white border-t border-neutral-800 pt-12 pb-10">
        <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
          {/* Top Footer Columns */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-8 pb-10 border-b border-neutral-800">
            <div className="md:col-span-4 flex items-start gap-4 text-neutral-300">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setLightboxFrame(SOCIAL_FRAMES[0])}
                aria-label="Instagram"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Instagram className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setLightboxFrame(SOCIAL_FRAMES[1])}
                aria-label="Share"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setLightboxFrame(SOCIAL_FRAMES[2])}
                aria-label="YouTube"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Youtube className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setInfoModal('contact')}
                aria-label="Email"
                className="hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-2 text-xs text-neutral-400">
              <p className="font-semibold text-white mb-2">Divisions</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('membership')}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                Membership ($29 to $99)
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('management')}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                NYC Model Management
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('media-production')}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                Media &amp; Visual Works
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setInfoModal('contact')}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                Contact &amp; Studio Line
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-2 text-xs text-neutral-400">
              <p className="font-semibold text-white mb-2">Community</p>
              <a href="#directory" className="block hover:text-white">
                Member Directory
              </a>
              <a href="#latest-work" className="block hover:text-white">
                Latest Work
              </a>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setJoinModalOpen(true)}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                Open Application
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setInfoModal('about')}
                className="block hover:text-white"
              >
                About Us
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-4">
              <p className="font-semibold text-xs text-white mb-2">Join Our Dispatch List</p>
              <form onSubmit={handleNewsletterSubmit} className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={newsletterEmail}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewsletterEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Email Address"
                  aria-label="Email Address"
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={newsletterStatus === 'submitting'}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
                >
                  {newsletterStatus === 'submitting' ? 'Saving...' : 'Sign Up'}
                </button>
              </form>
              {newsletterMessage && (
                <p
                  className={`mt-2 text-[11px] ${
                    newsletterStatus === 'error' ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-neutral-300'
                  }`}
                  role="status"
                >
                  {newsletterMessage}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Contact & Quick Actions */}
          <div className="pt-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
            <div className="text-xs text-neutral-400 space-y-1">
              <p className="font-semibold text-white">Contact &amp; Studio Line</p>
              <p>
                Direct Inquiries:{' '}
                <a
                  href={`mailto:${CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}`}
                  className="text-neutral-200 underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-white"
                >
                  {CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}
                </a>{' '}
                <span className="text-neutral-600" aria-hidden="true">
                  |
                </span>{' '}
                {CONTACT_CONFIG.officeLabel}:{' '}
                <span className="text-neutral-200">{CONTACT_CONFIG.officeAddress}</span>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleStartStripeCheckout(defaultTalentTier, 'monthly')}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Apply &amp; Pay Membership
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => scrollToSection('management')}
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-neutral-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:border-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Agency Bookings
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-neutral-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <span
              onDoubleClick={() => navigateTo('/admin')}
              className="font-display font-extrabold text-2xl tracking-[-0.04em] text-white"
            >
              nyc model club.
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
              NYC Model Club &amp; New York City Model Management · {CONTACT_CONFIG.officeAddress}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Modals */}
      <JoinDirectoryModal
        open={joinModalOpen}
        clientAccessUnlocked={clientAccessUnlocked}
        directoryMembers={allDirectoryMembers}
        onClose={() => setJoinModalOpen(false)}
        onProceedToMembership={() => handleStartStripeCheckout(defaultTalentTier, 'monthly')}
      />

      <StripeCheckoutModal
        tier={checkoutTier}
        billingCycle={checkoutCycle}
        sessionId={checkoutSessionId}
        paymentProvider={paymentProvider}
        onChangePaymentProvider={setPaymentProvider}
        onClose={() => setCheckoutTier(null)}
        onSuccess={(badge) => {
          setActiveMemberBadge({
            planName: badge.planName,
            memberCode: badge.memberCode,
          });
          if (badge.planId === 'clients') {
            sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_unlocked', '1');
            if (badge.sessionId) {
              sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_client_session_id', badge.sessionId);
            }
            setClientAccessUnlocked(true);
            refreshApprovedTalentAndDirectory(badge.sessionId);
          }
          if (badge.sessionId) {
            sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_last_session_id', badge.sessionId);
          }
          setCheckoutTier(null);
          navigateTo(
            `/onboarding?session_id=${encodeURIComponent(
              badge.sessionId
            )}&plan=${badge.planId}&cycle=${badge.billingCycle}`
          );
        }}
      />

      <EditorialStoryModal
        story={selectedStory}
        onClose={() => setSelectedStory(null)}
        onUnlockMembership={() => handleStartStripeCheckout(defaultTalentTier, 'monthly')}
      />

      <LookbookLightbox
        frame={lightboxFrame}
        onClose={() => setLightboxFrame(null)}
      />

      <InfoModal
        section={infoModal}
        onClose={() => setInfoModal(null)}
        onJumpToManagement={() => scrollToSection('management')}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

```

# src\components\AdminDashboard.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Lock, RefreshCw, Search, LogOut } from 'lucide-react';

interface AdminMember {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: string;
  plan_id: string;
  billing_cycle: string;
  amount_cents: number;
  status: string;
  stripe_session_id: string | null;
  date_of_birth?: string;
  is_minor?: number;
  guardian_name?: string;
  guardian_relationship?: string;
  guardian_contact?: string;
  profile_visibility?: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  created_at: string;
}

interface AdminBooking {
  id: string;
  booking_scope?: 'model' | 'production' | 'bundle';
  model_id: string;
  model_name: string;
  package_label: string;
  production_package?: string;
  base_rate_dollars: number;
  client_fee_dollars: number;
  model_commission_dollars: number;
  total_client_charge_dollars: number;
  model_net_payout_dollars: number;
  client_name: string;
  client_email: string;
  shoot_date: string;
  notes: string;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
}

interface AdminApplication {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: string;
  borough: string;
  email: string;
  instagram: string;
  date_of_birth?: string;
  is_minor?: number;
  guardian_name?: string;
  guardian_relationship?: string;
  guardian_contact?: string;
  guardian_consent?: number;
  profile_visibility?: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected';
  created_at: string;
}

interface AdminNewsletter {
  email: string;
  created_at: string;
}

interface AdminOnboarding {
  id: string;
  member_id: string | null;
  stripe_session_id: string | null;
  plan_id: 'talent' | 'studio' | 'clients';
  billing_cycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  stage_name: string;
  measurements: string;
  location: string;
  portfolio_url: string;
  digitals_url: string;
  studio_shoot_slot: string;
  company_name: string;
  company_role: string;
  project_type: string;
  date_of_birth?: string;
  is_minor?: number;
  guardian_name?: string;
  guardian_relationship?: string;
  guardian_contact?: string;
  guardian_consent?: number;
  profile_visibility?: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  review_status: 'new' | 'scouting' | 'approved_to_roster' | 'archived';
  confirmation_notified_at: string | null;
  confirmation_channel: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface AdminDataResponse {
  members: AdminMember[];
  bookings: AdminBooking[];
  applications: AdminApplication[];
  newsletterSubscribers: AdminNewsletter[];
  onboarding?: AdminOnboarding[];
  stats: {
    activeMembersCount: number;
    totalBookingsCount: number;
    paidBookingsCount: number;
    pendingApplicationsCount: number;
    newsletterCount: number;
    newOnboardingCount?: number;
    totalOnboardingCount?: number;
  };
}

type AdminTab = 'onboarding' | 'members' | 'bookings' | 'applications' | 'newsletter';

interface AdminDashboardProps {
  onNavigateHome: () => void;
}

const EMAIL_STORAGE_KEY = 'nycmc_admin_email';
const PASS_STORAGE_KEY = 'nycmc_admin_password';

const PLAN_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  talent: 'Model & Talent ($29)',
  studio: 'Studio Access ($59)',
  clients: 'Clients & Agencies ($99)',
};

function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  try {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  } catch {
    return iso;
  }
}

export const AdminDashboard: React.FC<AdminDashboardProps> = ({ onNavigateHome }) => {
  const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState('');
  const [passwordInput, setPasswordInput] = useState('');
  const [adminEmail, setAdminEmail] = useState<string>(
    () => sessionStorage.getItem(EMAIL_STORAGE_KEY) || ''
  );
  const [adminPassword, setAdminPassword] = useState<string>(
    () => sessionStorage.getItem(PASS_STORAGE_KEY) || ''
  );
  const [data, setData] = useState<AdminDataResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AdminTab>('onboarding');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [updatingAppId, setUpdatingAppId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [updatingOnboardingId, setUpdatingOnboardingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [previewDigital, setPreviewDigital] = useState<{
    name: string;
    src: string;
    meta: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const fetchAdminData = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setAuthError(null);
    setActionError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/data', {
        headers: {
          'X-Admin-Email': email.trim(),
          'X-Admin-Password': password,
        },
      });
      const payload = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as Partial<AdminDataResponse> & {
        error?: string;
      };
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (res.status === 401) {
          sessionStorage.removeItem(EMAIL_STORAGE_KEY);
          sessionStorage.removeItem(PASS_STORAGE_KEY);
          setAdminEmail('');
          setAdminPassword('');
          setData(null);
        }
        setAuthError(payload.error || 'Authentication failed.');
        return;
      }

      sessionStorage.setItem(EMAIL_STORAGE_KEY, email.trim());
      sessionStorage.setItem(PASS_STORAGE_KEY, password);
      setAdminEmail(email.trim());
      setAdminPassword(password);
      setData(payload as AdminDataResponse);
    } catch {
      setAuthError('Unable to connect to the admin server endpoint.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (adminEmail && adminPassword) {
      void fetchAdminData(adminEmail, adminPassword);
    }
  }, [adminEmail, adminPassword, fetchAdminData]);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!emailInput.trim() || !passwordInput) {
      setAuthError('Please enter both your admin email and password.');
      return;
    }
    await fetchAdminData(emailInput.trim(), passwordInput);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(EMAIL_STORAGE_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(PASS_STORAGE_KEY);
    setAdminEmail('');
    setAdminPassword('');
    setEmailInput('');
    setPasswordInput('');
    setData(null);
    setAuthError(null);
  };

  const handleApplicationStatus = async (
    id: string,
    nextStatus: 'approved' | 'rejected' | 'pending'
  ) => {
    if (!adminEmail || !adminPassword) return;
    setUpdatingAppId(id);
    setActionError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/applications/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-Admin-Email': adminEmail,
          'X-Admin-Password': adminPassword,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ status: nextStatus }),
      });
      const payload = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) {
        setActionError(payload.error || 'Failed to update application status.');
        return;
      }

      setData((prev) => {
        if (!prev) return prev;
        const updatedApps = prev.applications.map((app) =>
          app.id === id ? { ...app, status: nextStatus } : app
        );
        return {
          ...prev,
          applications: updatedApps,
          stats: {
            ...prev.stats,
            pendingApplicationsCount: updatedApps.filter((a) => a.status === 'pending').length,
          },
        };
      });
    } catch {
      setActionError('Network error updating application.');
    } finally {
      setUpdatingAppId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleOnboardingReviewStatus = async (
    id: string,
    reviewStatus: AdminOnboarding['review_status']
  ) => {
    if (!adminEmail || !adminPassword) return;
    setUpdatingOnboardingId(id);
    setActionError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/onboarding/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'X-Admin-Email': adminEmail,
          'X-Admin-Password': adminPassword,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ reviewStatus }),
      });
      const payload = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { error?: string };
      if (!res.ok) {
        setActionError(payload.error || 'Failed to update onboarding review status.');
        return;
      }

      setData((prev) => {
        if (!prev) return prev;
        const updatedOnboarding = (prev.onboarding || []).map((item) =>
          item.id === id ? { ...item, review_status: reviewStatus } : item
        );
        return {
          ...prev,
          onboarding: updatedOnboarding,
          stats: {
            ...prev.stats,
            newOnboardingCount: updatedOnboarding.filter((o) => o.review_status === 'new').length,
            totalOnboardingCount: updatedOnboarding.length,
          },
        };
      });
    } catch {
      setActionError('Network error updating onboarding profile.');
    } finally {
      setUpdatingOnboardingId(null);
    }
  };

  const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

  const filteredOnboarding = useMemo(() => {
    const list = data?.onboarding || [];
    if (!q) return list;
    return list.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.stage_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.company_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.location.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.plan_id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        o.review_status.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [data, q]);

  const filteredMembers = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return [];
    if (!q) return data.members;
    return data.members.filter(
      (m) =>
        m.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        m.email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        m.plan_id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [data, q]);

  const filteredBookings = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return [];
    if (!q) return data.bookings;
    return data.bookings.filter(
      (b) =>
        b.model_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        b.client_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        b.client_email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        b.id.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [data, q]);

  const filteredApplications = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return [];
    if (!q) return data.applications;
    return data.applications.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        a.email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        a.borough.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        a.role.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        a.status.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [data, q]);

  const filteredNewsletter = useMemo(() => {
    if (!data) return [];
    if (!q) return data.newsletterSubscribers;
    return data.newsletterSubscribers.filter((n) => n.email.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }, [data, q]);

  if (!data) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-white text-black flex flex-col">
        <header className="border-b border-neutral-200 h-14 px-6 md:px-10 flex items-center justify-between gap-8">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
            <span className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-[-0.04em]">
              nyc model club.
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">/</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-600">
              Admin Desk
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onNavigateHome}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:text-black whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>Back to Site</span>
          </button>
        </header>

        <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-6">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md border border-black bg-white p-8 md:p-10">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Protected Agency Desk</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl mt-2 text-black">
              Administration Sign In.
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
              Sign in with your agency administrator email and password to inspect paid members,
              online model bookings, talent applications, and dispatch subscribers.
            </p>

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="admin-email-input"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                >
                  Admin Email
                </label>
                <input
                  id="admin-email-input"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={emailInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmailInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="admin@nycmodelclub.com"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="admin-password-input"
                  className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                >
                  Admin Password
                </label>
                <input
                  id="admin-password-input"
                  type="password"
                  required
                  value={passwordInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setPasswordInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter admin password"
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              {authError && (
                <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
                  {authError}
                </p>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
              >
                {loading ? 'Verifying Credentials...' : 'Unlock Admin Desk'}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-white text-black flex flex-col">
      {/* Top Bar */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-xs border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div className="max-w-[1280px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 h-14 flex items-center justify-between gap-8">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
            <a
              href="/"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                onNavigateHome();
              }}
              className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-[-0.04em] text-black"
            >
              nyc model club.
            </a>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">/</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-600">
              Admin Desk
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void fetchAdminData(adminEmail, adminPassword)}
              disabled={loading}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 border border-neutral-300 text-xs font-medium text-black hover:border-black transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              <RefreshCw
                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
              <span>Refresh</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onNavigateHome}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 border border-neutral-300 text-xs font-medium text-black hover:border-black transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Site</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleLogout}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-medium hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>Sign Out</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-[1280px] w-full mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-10">
        {/* Heading */}
        <div className="pb-8 border-b border-neutral-200 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-neutral-500">
              SQLite Persistent Ledger
            </p>
            <h1 className="font-editorial italic text-4xl md:text-5xl text-black mt-1">
              Club &amp; Management Operations.
            </h1>
          </div>

          <div className="relative w-full md:w-72">
            <Search
              className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2"
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
            <input
              type="search"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Filter name, email, ID..."
              aria-label="Filter admin records"
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 border border-neutral-300 text-xs focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 5 Summary Metrics */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 border-x border-b border-neutral-200 divide-y lg:divide-y-0 divide-x divide-neutral-200">
          <div className="p-6">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              New Onboarding
            </p>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-3xl text-black mt-2 tabular-nums">
              {data.onboarding?.length || 0}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1 tabular-nums">
              {data.stats.newOnboardingCount || 0} awaiting scout
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-6">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Paid Members
            </p>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-3xl text-black mt-2 tabular-nums">
              {data.members.length}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1 tabular-nums">
              {data.stats.activeMembersCount} active passes
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-6">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Model Bookings
            </p>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-3xl text-black mt-2 tabular-nums">
              {data.bookings.length}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1 tabular-nums">
              {data.stats.paidBookingsCount} paid online
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-6">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Talent Applications
            </p>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-3xl text-black mt-2 tabular-nums">
              {data.applications.length}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1 tabular-nums">
              {data.stats.pendingApplicationsCount} pending review
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-6 col-span-2 lg:col-span-1">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
              Dispatch Subscribers
            </p>
            <p className="font-display font-bold text-3xl text-black mt-2 tabular-nums">
              {data.newsletterSubscribers.length}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1">Newsletter list</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation Tabs */}
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 p-1 bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200 w-fit">
          {(
            [
              {
                id: 'onboarding',
                label: `New Members & Onboarding (${filteredOnboarding.length})`,
              },
              { id: 'members', label: `Paid Members (${filteredMembers.length})` },
              { id: 'bookings', label: `Model Bookings (${filteredBookings.length})` },
              { id: 'applications', label: `Talent Applications (${filteredApplications.length})` },
              { id: 'newsletter', label: `Newsletter (${filteredNewsletter.length})` },
            ] as const
          ).map((tab) => {
            const active = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 tabular-nums cursor-pointer ${
                  active ? 'bg-black text-white' : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {actionError && (
          <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
            {actionError}
          </p>
        )}

        {/* Data Tables */}
        <div className="mt-6 border border-neutral-200 overflow-x-auto">
          {activeTab === 'onboarding' && (
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-500 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Ref / Tier</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Member / Company</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Profile &amp; Digitals</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Studio Shoot / Project</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Agency Action</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                {filteredOnboarding.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={6} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-neutral-500">
                      No onboarding profiles submitted yet. Post-payment submissions from
                      /onboarding appear here immediately.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredOnboarding.map((ob) => {
                    const isUpdating = updatingOnboardingId === ob.id;
                    const isClient = ob.plan_id === 'clients';
                    const displayTitle = isClient
                      ? ob.company_name || ob.full_name
                      : ob.stage_name
                        ? `${ob.full_name} (${ob.stage_name})`
                        : ob.full_name;

                    return (
                      <tr key={ob.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors align-top">
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                          <span className="font-mono text-black font-semibold block">{ob.id}</span>
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 block mt-0.5">
                            {PLAN_LABELS[ob.plan_id] || ob.plan_id}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-400 uppercase block">
                            {ob.billing_cycle}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4">
                          <span className="font-medium text-black block">{displayTitle}</span>
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 block">{ob.email}</span>
                          {ob.location && (
                            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 block">{ob.location}</span>
                          )}
                          {ob.date_of_birth && (
                            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500 block">
                              DOB: {ob.date_of_birth}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {(ob.is_minor === 1 ||
                            ob.profile_visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                            ob.visibility_scope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                            <div className="mt-1 px-2 py-1 border border-black bg-neutral-100 text-[10px] font-mono text-black">
                              <span className="block font-semibold uppercase">
                                Minor (Guardian Required)
                              </span>
                              <span className="block text-neutral-600">
                                Visibility: Verified Clients &amp; Agencies Only
                              </span>
                              {ob.guardian_name && (
                                <span className="block text-neutral-700">
                                  {ob.guardian_relationship || 'Guardian'}: {ob.guardian_name} (
                                  {ob.guardian_contact})
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          )}
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-neutral-400 block mt-0.5">
                            {formatDate(ob.created_at)}
                            {ob.confirmation_notified_at ? ' · Notified' : ''}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4">
                          {isClient ? (
                            <div className="space-y-0.5">
                              <span className="text-black font-medium block">
                                {ob.company_role}
                              </span>
                              {ob.full_name && ob.full_name !== ob.company_name && (
                                <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 block">
                                  Contact: {ob.full_name}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                              {ob.digitals_url && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    setPreviewDigital({
                                      name: displayTitle,
                                      src: ob.digitals_url,
                                      meta: `${ob.measurements} · ${ob.location}`,
                                    })
                                  }
                                  className="shrink-0 border border-neutral-300 hover:border-black transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                  title="Inspect headshot"
                                >
                                  <img
                                    src={ob.digitals_url}
                                    alt={displayTitle}
                                    className="w-10 h-12 object-cover"
                                  />
                                </button>
                              )}
                              <div className="space-y-0.5 min-w-0">
                                {ob.measurements && (
                                  <span className="text-black block">{ob.measurements}</span>
                                )}
                                {ob.portfolio_url && (
                                  <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-600 block truncate max-w-[220px]">
                                    {ob.portfolio_url}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700 max-w-[220px]">
                          {ob.plan_id === 'studio' && ob.studio_shoot_slot ? (
                            <span>{ob.studio_shoot_slot}</span>
                          ) : isClient && ob.project_type ? (
                            <span>{ob.project_type}</span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-neutral-400">—</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono uppercase text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap">
                          {ob.review_status === 'approved_to_roster'
                            ? 'Roster Approved'
                            : ob.review_status}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="inline-flex items-center border border-neutral-300 divide-x divide-neutral-300">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={isUpdating}
                              onClick={() =>
                                void handleOnboardingReviewStatus(
                                  ob.id,
                                  ob.review_status === 'scouting' ? 'new' : 'scouting'
                                )
                              }
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${
                                ob.review_status === 'scouting'
                                  ? 'bg-neutral-800 text-white'
                                  : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-100'
                              }`}
                            >
                              Scout
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={isUpdating}
                              onClick={() =>
                                void handleOnboardingReviewStatus(
                                  ob.id,
                                  ob.review_status === 'approved_to_roster'
                                    ? 'new'
                                    : 'approved_to_roster'
                                )
                              }
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${
                                ob.review_status === 'approved_to_roster'
                                  ? 'bg-black text-white'
                                  : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-100'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {ob.review_status === 'approved_to_roster'
                                ? 'On Roster'
                                : 'Approve Roster'}
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={isUpdating}
                              onClick={() =>
                                void handleOnboardingReviewStatus(
                                  ob.id,
                                  ob.review_status === 'archived' ? 'new' : 'archived'
                                )
                              }
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${
                                ob.review_status === 'archived'
                                  ? 'bg-neutral-700 text-white'
                                  : 'bg-white text-neutral-500 hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:text-black'
                              }`}
                            >
                              Archive
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
          {activeTab === 'members' && (
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-500 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Pass ID</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Member</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Email</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Plan Tier</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Billing</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Amount</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Date</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                {filteredMembers.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={8} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-neutral-500">
                      No paid members recorded yet. Completed Stripe memberships appear here
                      automatically.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredMembers.map((m) => (
                    <tr key={m.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-black whitespace-nowrap">{m.id}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-black">
                        {m.full_name}
                        <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 font-normal">
                          {m.role}
                        </span>
                        {m.date_of_birth && (
                          <span className="block text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500 font-normal">
                            DOB: {m.date_of_birth}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {(m.is_minor === 1 ||
                          m.profile_visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                          m.visibility_scope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                          <div className="mt-1 px-2 py-1 border border-black bg-neutral-100 text-[10px] font-mono text-black font-normal">
                            <span className="block font-semibold uppercase">
                              Minor (Guardian Required)
                            </span>
                            <span className="block text-neutral-600">
                              Visibility: Verified Clients &amp; Agencies Only
                            </span>
                            {m.guardian_name && (
                              <span className="block text-neutral-700">
                                {m.guardian_relationship || 'Guardian'}: {m.guardian_name} (
                                {m.guardian_contact})
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700">{m.email}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-black">
                        {PLAN_LABELS[m.plan_id] || m.plan_id}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 capitalize text-neutral-600">{m.billing_cycle}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-black">
                        ${(m.amount_cents / 100).toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono uppercase text-[11px]">
                        {m.status === 'active' ? 'Active' : m.status}
                      </td>
```
