```tsx
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatDate(m.created_at)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'bookings' && (
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-500 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Ref</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Talent</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Package</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Client</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Shoot Date</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Client Total (+20%)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Model Net (-20%)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Created</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                {filteredBookings.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={9} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-neutral-500">
                      No model bookings recorded yet. Online bookings from New York City Model
                      Management appear here.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredBookings.map((b) => (
                    <tr key={b.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-black whitespace-nowrap">{b.id}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-black">
                        {b.model_name}
                        <span className="block text-[11px] font-normal text-neutral-500">
                          {b.booking_scope === 'production'
                            ? 'Media Production Only'
                            : b.booking_scope === 'bundle'
                              ? 'Bundle: Model + Media'
                              : 'Models Only'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700">{b.package_label}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-black">
                        {b.client_name}
                        <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{b.client_email}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-600">{b.shoot_date}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-black">
                        ${b.total_client_charge_dollars.toLocaleString()}.00
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-neutral-600">
                        ${b.model_net_payout_dollars.toLocaleString()}.00
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono uppercase text-[11px]">
                        {b.status === 'paid' ? 'Paid' : 'Pending'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                        {formatDate(b.created_at)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'applications' && (
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-500 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">ID</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Applicant</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Discipline</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Borough</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Contact</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Status</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Review Decision</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                {filteredApplications.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={7} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-neutral-500">
                      No talent applications submitted yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredApplications.map((app) => {
                    const isUpdating = updatingAppId === app.id;
                    return (
                      <tr key={app.id} className="hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors">
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-black whitespace-nowrap">
                          {app.id}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-black">
                          {app.full_name}
                          {app.date_of_birth && (
                            <span className="block text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500 font-normal">
                              DOB: {app.date_of_birth}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {(app.is_minor === 1 ||
                            app.profile_visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                            app.visibility_scope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                            <div className="mt-1 px-2 py-1 border border-black bg-neutral-100 text-[10px] font-mono text-black font-normal">
                              <span className="block font-semibold uppercase">
                                Minor (Guardian Required)
                              </span>
                              <span className="block text-neutral-600">
                                Visibility: Verified Clients &amp; Agencies Only
                              </span>
                              {app.guardian_name && (
                                <span className="block text-neutral-700">
                                  {app.guardian_relationship || 'Guardian'}: {app.guardian_name} (
                                  {app.guardian_contact})
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          )}
                          <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 font-normal">
                            {formatDate(app.created_at)}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700">{app.role}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-600">{app.borough}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700">
                          {app.email}
                          {app.instagram && (
                            <span className="block text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500">
                              {app.instagram}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono uppercase text-[11px]">
                          {app.status}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                          <div className="inline-flex items-center border border-neutral-300 divide-x divide-neutral-300">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={isUpdating}
                              onClick={() =>
                                void handleApplicationStatus(
                                  app.id,
                                  app.status === 'approved' ? 'pending' : 'approved'
                                )
                              }
                              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                                app.status === 'approved'
                                  ? 'bg-black text-white'
                                  : 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-100'
                              }`}
                            >
                              Approve
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={isUpdating}
                              onClick={() =>
                                void handleApplicationStatus(
                                  app.id,
                                  app.status === 'rejected' ? 'pending' : 'rejected'
                                )
                              }
                              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
                                app.status === 'rejected'
                                  ? 'bg-neutral-900 text-white'
                                  : 'bg-white text-neutral-600 hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:text-black'
                              }`}
                            >
                              Reject
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'newsletter' && (
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-500 font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">Subscriber Email</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-right">Subscribed Date</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                {filteredNewsletter.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={2} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-neutral-500">
                      No newsletter subscribers yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredNewsletter.map((sub) => (
                    <tr key={sub.email} className="hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium text-black">{sub.email}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-right text-neutral-500">
                        {formatDate(sub.created_at)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {previewDigital && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-label="Digital headshot preview"
          onClick={() => setPreviewDigital(null)}
        >
          <div
            className="bg-white text-black border border-black max-w-md w-full p-5"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-3 border-b border-neutral-200">
              <div>
                <p className="font-display font-bold text-base text-black">{previewDigital.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">{previewDigital.meta}</p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setPreviewDigital(null)}
                className="px-3 py-1 border border-neutral-300 text-xs font-semibold hover:border-black cursor-pointer"
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
            <img
              src={previewDigital.src}
              alt={previewDigital.name}
              className="mt-4 w-full max-h-[65vh] object-contain bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

```

# src\components\EditorialPlate.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PlateVariant } from '../data/clubData';

interface EditorialPlateProps {
  variant: PlateVariant;
  className?: string;
  darkSurface?: boolean;
  label?: string;
}

const PHOTO_MAP: Record<PlateVariant, string> = {
  hero_editorial: '/assets/aistudio/image_be9b51bd.png',
  soren: '/assets/pro_news_2.png',
  nadia: '/assets/trending_1.png',
  kaelen: '/assets/trending_3.png',
  julian: '/assets/trending_2.png',
  claire: '/assets/trending_6.png',
  mateo: '/assets/trending_4.png',
  beach: '/assets/trending_5.png',
  cover_numero: '/assets/trending_3.png',
  studio_group: '/assets/pro_news_4.png',
  campaign_warm: '/assets/pro_news_1.png',
  runway_red: '/assets/pro_news_3.png',
};

/**
 * Editorial fashion & portrait plates rendering real high-contrast monochrome
 * photography with bespoke vector chiaroscuro fallback containers.
 */
export const EditorialPlate: React.FC<EditorialPlateProps> = ({
  variant,
  className = '',
  darkSurface = false,
  label,
}) => {
  const [imgError, setImgError] = useState(false);
  const photoSrc = PHOTO_MAP[variant];
  const configs: Record<
    PlateVariant,
    {
      bg: string;
      lightBeam: string;
      silhouette: string;
      filmMark: string;
      lightText?: boolean;
    }
  > = {
    hero_editorial: {
      bg: 'from-[#d8d3cc] via-[#bfb7ad] to-[#7a736b]',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,248,240,0.65)',
      silhouette: '#171514',
      filmMark: 'NYC MODEL CLUB',
    },
    soren: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-200 via-neutral-300 to-neutral-500',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)',
      silhouette: '#111111',
      filmMark: 'TRI-X 400 · 01A',
    },
    nadia: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-100 via-neutral-200 to-neutral-400',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)',
      silhouette: '#0d0d0d',
      filmMark: 'ILFORD HP5 · 02B',
    },
    kaelen: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-800 via-neutral-900 to-black',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)',
      silhouette: '#090909',
      filmMark: 'STUDIO · 03C',
      lightText: true,
    },
    julian: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-300 via-neutral-400 to-neutral-600',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)',
      silhouette: '#141414',
      filmMark: 'SOHO 35MM · 04D',
    },
    claire: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-700 via-neutral-800 to-neutral-950',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)',
      silhouette: '#0c0c0c',
      filmMark: 'CHELSEA · 05E',
      lightText: true,
    },
    mateo: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-200 via-neutral-400 to-neutral-700',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
      silhouette: '#101010',
      filmMark: 'TRIBECA · 06F',
    },
    beach: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-100 via-neutral-300 to-neutral-500',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)',
      silhouette: '#111111',
      filmMark: 'DAYLIGHT · 07G',
    },
    cover_numero: {
      bg: 'from-[#c8c0b4] via-[#a79c8e] to-[#595147]',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,250,242,0.55)',
      silhouette: '#161311',
      filmMark: 'ISSUE 12 · NY',
    },
    studio_group: {
      bg: 'from-neutral-800 via-neutral-900 to-black',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)',
      silhouette: '#050505',
      filmMark: 'CROSBY ST · 08H',
      lightText: true,
    },
    campaign_warm: {
      bg: 'from-[#cfc9be] via-[#aea699] to-[#635d54]',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
      silhouette: '#181818',
      filmMark: 'BACKSTAGE · 09J',
    },
    runway_red: {
      bg: 'from-[#3a1618] via-[#220c0e] to-[#0a0405]',
      lightBeam: 'rgba(255,215,215,0.2)',
      silhouette: '#0d0607',
      filmMark: 'RUNWAY · 10K',
      lightText: true,
    },
  };

  const c = configs[variant];

  if (photoSrc && !imgError) {
    return (
      <div
        className={`relative overflow-hidden bg-neutral-900 select-none ${className}`}
        role="img"
        aria-label={label || `Editorial fashion plate ${variant}`}
      >
        <img
          src={photoSrc}
          alt={label || `Editorial fashion plate ${variant}`}
          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
          onError={() => setImgError(true)}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top"
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-b ${c.bg} select-none ${className}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={label || `Editorial fashion plate ${variant}`}
    >
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
        style={{
          background: `radial-gradient(circle at 28% 22%, ${c.lightBeam} 0%, transparent 62%)`,
        }}
      />

      {variant === 'hero_editorial' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 800 450" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <rect width="800" height="450" fill="none" />
          {/* Architectural studio backdrop */}
          <rect x="0" y="0" width="290" height="450" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.18)" />
          <line x1="290" y1="0" x2="290" y2="450" stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.12)" strokeWidth="1" />
          {/* Off-center editorial portrait bust */}
          <ellipse cx="430" cy="145" rx="56" ry="72" fill="#2b2623" />
          <path d="M372,135 C374,72 486,70 488,135 C466,104 394,104 372,135 Z" fill="#12100f" />
          <path d="M430,74 C462,74 486,104 486,144 C486,186 462,216 430,216 Z" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.34)" />
          <rect x="406" y="208" width="48" height="58" fill="#231f1d" />
          {/* Sculptural draped editorial gown/coat */}
          <path
            d="M270,312 C320,270 374,258 406,256 L430,322 L454,256 C486,258 545,270 595,312 L630,450 L235,450 Z"
            fill="#141210"
          />
          <polygon points="406,256 430,335 365,305" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.15)" />
        </svg>
      ) : variant === 'studio_group' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 800 450" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          {/* Loft studio cyclorama windows */}
          <rect x="80" y="40" width="140" height="210" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.2)" />
          <rect x="245" y="40" width="140" height="210" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.18)" />
          <rect x="410" y="40" width="140" height="210" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.2)" />
          {/* Three standing figures in studio */}
          <circle cx="260" cy="182" r="22" fill="#262626" />
          <path d="M210,224 C230,206 290,206 310,224 L322,450 L198,450 Z" fill="#0e0e0e" stroke="#2e2e2e" />
          <circle cx="400" cy="168" r="24" fill="#323232" />
          <path d="M342,212 C368,194 432,194 458,212 L472,450 L328,450 Z" fill="#171717" stroke="#3f3f46" />
          <circle cx="540" cy="186" r="21" fill="#222222" />
          <path d="M492,226 C514,210 566,210 588,226 L600,450 L480,450 Z" fill="#090909" stroke="#27272a" />
        </svg>
      ) : variant === 'cover_numero' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 300 400" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <ellipse cx="150" cy="132" rx="39" ry="50" fill="#2c2722" />
          <path d="M111,122 C112,76 188,76 189,122 C170,98 130,98 111,122 Z" fill="#14110f" />
          <rect x="134" y="176" width="32" height="44" fill="#26221e" />
          <path d="M58,242 C92,214 126,208 150,208 C174,208 208,214 242,242 L262,400 L38,400 Z" fill="#171412" />
          <text x="20" y="54" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)" fontFamily="Georgia, serif" fontSize="34" fontStyle="italic">
            12
          </text>
        </svg>
      ) : variant === 'beach' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 400 300" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <rect width="400" height="210" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.28)" />
          <rect y="210" width="400" height="90" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.08)" />
          <line x1="0" y1="210" x2="400" y2="210" stroke="#737373" strokeWidth="1" />
          <polygon points="150,70 295,88 295,210 150,210" fill="#fafafa" stroke="#262626" strokeWidth="1.2" />
          <polygon points="150,70 220,56 295,88" fill="#e5e5e5" stroke="#262626" strokeWidth="1" />
          <rect x="200" y="108" width="42" height="102" fill="#171717" />
          <circle cx="220" cy="128" r="7" fill="#0a0a0a" />
          <path d="M214,136 L226,136 L232,192 L209,192 Z" fill="#0a0a0a" />
          <line x1="216" y1="192" x2="214" y2="210" stroke="#0a0a0a" strokeWidth="2.5" />
          <line x1="224" y1="192" x2="227" y2="210" stroke="#0a0a0a" strokeWidth="2.5" />
          <polygon points="208,210 232,210 182,250 162,250" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.22)" />
        </svg>
      ) : variant === 'nadia' || variant === 'claire' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 300 400" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <polygon
            points="175,0 300,0 300,400 110,400"
            fill={variant === 'claire' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.08)'}
          />
          <ellipse cx="150" cy="128" rx="38" ry="49" fill={variant === 'claire' ? '#d4d4d8' : '#262626'} />
          <path
            d="M112,118 C112,76 188,76 188,118 C188,100 170,84 150,84 C130,84 112,100 112,118 Z"
            fill="#090909"
          />
          <path
            d="M150,86 C171,86 186,104 186,130 C186,156 170,176 150,176 Z"
            fill={variant === 'claire' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'}
          />
          <rect x="136" y="170" width="28" height="44" fill={variant === 'claire' ? '#a1a1aa' : '#262626'} />
          <path
            d="M54,238 C78,214 114,206 136,204 L150,256 L164,204 C186,206 222,214 246,238 L268,400 L32,400 Z"
            fill={c.silhouette}
          />
        </svg>
      ) : variant === 'kaelen' || variant === 'runway_red' ? (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 300 400" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <polygon points="0,0 165,0 95,400 0,400" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" />
          <ellipse cx="152" cy="126" rx="37" ry="47" fill="#27272a" />
          <path d="M114,116 C115,74 188,72 190,118 C176,92 130,90 114,116 Z" fill="#050505" />
          <path d="M116,120 C116,96 132,84 148,84 L148,172 C130,170 116,150 116,120 Z" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.24)" />
          <rect x="133" y="168" width="38" height="42" rx="4" fill="#18181b" stroke="#3f3f46" strokeWidth="1" />
          <path
            d="M64,228 C94,208 122,202 150,202 C178,202 206,208 236,228 L256,400 L44,400 Z"
            fill="#0a0a0a"
            stroke="#27272a"
            strokeWidth="1"
          />
        </svg>
      ) : (
        <svg viewBox="0 0 300 400" className="w-full h-full object-cover" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">
          <rect x="24" y="24" width="110" height="220" fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.22)" />
          <ellipse cx="152" cy="122" rx="35" ry="46" fill="#262626" />
          <path d="M116,112 C118,74 186,74 188,112 C176,95 130,92 116,112 Z" fill="#090909" />
          <path d="M152,78 C174,78 187,98 187,124 C187,150 172,168 152,168 Z" fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.42)" />
          <rect x="135" y="162" width="34" height="38" fill="#1f1f1f" />
          <path
            d="M46,232 C76,206 112,196 135,194 L152,248 L169,194 C192,196 228,206 254,232 L274,400 L26,400 Z"
            fill="#0a0a0a"
          />
          <polygon points="122,196 146,194 148,258 112,248" fill="#171717" stroke="#404040" strokeWidth="0.8" />
          <polygon points="182,196 158,194 156,258 192,248" fill="#171717" stroke="#404040" strokeWidth="0.8" />
        </svg>
      )}

      <div className="absolute inset-0 film-grain opacity-40 pointer-events-none" />

      <div
        className={`absolute bottom-2.5 left-3 font-mono text-[10px] tracking-widest uppercase pointer-events-none ${
          darkSurface || c.lightText ? 'text-white/55' : 'text-black/45'
        }`}
      >
        {c.filmMark}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

# src\components\InteractiveModals.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  CONTACT_CONFIG,
  CreativeRoleId,
  DIRECTORY_MEMBERS,
  DirectoryMember,
  EditorialStory,
  ROLE_OPTIONS,
  SocialFrame,
  calculateExactAge,
} from '../data/clubData';
import { EditorialPlate } from './EditorialPlate';

interface JoinDirectoryModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  defaultRole?: CreativeRoleId;
  clientAccessUnlocked?: boolean;
  directoryMembers?: DirectoryMember[];
  onClose: () => void;
  onProceedToMembership: () => void;
}

export const JoinDirectoryModal: React.FC<JoinDirectoryModalProps> = ({
  open,
  defaultRole = 'model',
  clientAccessUnlocked = false,
  directoryMembers,
  onClose,
  onProceedToMembership,
}) => {
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<string>(
    ROLE_OPTIONS.find((r) => r.id === defaultRole)?.label || 'Model'
  );
  const [borough, setBorough] = useState('Manhattan, NY');
  const [instagram, setInstagram] = useState('');
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState('');
  const [guardianName, setGuardianName] = useState('');
  const [guardianRelationship, setGuardianRelationship] = useState('');
  const [guardianContact, setGuardianContact] = useState('');
  const [guardianConsent, setGuardianConsent] = useState(false);
  const [submittedAsMinor, setSubmittedAsMinor] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [createdId, setCreatedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [directoryFilter, setDirectoryFilter] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setRole(ROLE_OPTIONS.find((r) => r.id === defaultRole)?.label || 'Model');
      setCreatedId(null);
      setErrorMsg(null);
      setSubmittedAsMinor(false);
    }
  }, [open, defaultRole]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const calculatedAge = calculateExactAge(dateOfBirth);
  const isMinor = calculatedAge !== null && calculatedAge < 18;
  const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  const baseMembers = directoryMembers || DIRECTORY_MEMBERS;
  const visibleDirectoryMembers = baseMembers.filter(
    (m) =>
      clientAccessUnlocked ||
      (!m.isMinor &&
        m.visibility !== 'clients_only' &&
        m.visibilityScope !== 'verified_clients_only')
  );

  const filteredMembers = visibleDirectoryMembers.filter(
    (m) =>
      m.name.toLowerCase().includes(directoryFilter.toLowerCase()) ||
      m.role.toLowerCase().includes(directoryFilter.toLowerCase()) ||
      m.location.toLowerCase().includes(directoryFilter.toLowerCase())
  );

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg(null);

    if (calculatedAge === null) {
      setErrorMsg('Please enter a valid Date of Birth.');
      return;
    }

    if (isMinor) {
      if (guardianName.trim().length < 2) {
        setErrorMsg(
          'Applicants under 18 (minors) must be registered by a parent or legal guardian/tutor. Please enter their full name.'
        );
        return;
      }
      if (!guardianRelationship) {
        setErrorMsg(
          'Please select the guardian relationship (Parent, Legal Guardian, or Legal Tutor).'
        );
        return;
      }
      if (guardianContact.trim().length < 4) {
        setErrorMsg(
          'Please enter parent or legal guardian/tutor contact details (email or phone).'
        );
        return;
      }
      if (!guardianConsent) {
        setErrorMsg(
          'Please confirm parent or legal guardian/tutor registration and private Clients & Agencies visibility.'
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/applications', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          fullName,
          email,
          role,
          borough,
          instagram,
          dateOfBirth,
          isMinor,
          visibilityScope: isMinor ? 'verified_clients_only' : 'public',
          guardianName: isMinor ? guardianName.trim() : '',
          guardianRelationship: isMinor ? guardianRelationship : '',
          guardianContact: isMinor ? guardianContact.trim() : '',
          guardianConsent: isMinor && guardianConsent,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setErrorMsg(data.error || 'Submission failed.');
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }
      setSubmittedAsMinor(isMinor);
      setCreatedId(data.profileId);
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg('Network error submitting application.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="join-modal-title"
    >
      <div className="bg-white text-black border border-black w-full max-w-3xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto shadow-2xl">
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-600">
            NYC Model Club · Open Application &amp; Directory
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close modal"
            className="p-1 text-neutral-500 hover:text-black transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 md:p-8 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
          <div>
            <h3 id="join-modal-title" className="font-display font-bold text-2xl tracking-tight">
              Apply to NYC Model Club
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
              The club is open to everybody applying and paying the membership fee. Submit your
              application details below, then activate your Stripe membership pass.
            </p>

            {createdId ? (
              <div className="mt-6 p-5 border border-black bg-neutral-50 space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-sm">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Application Registered ({createdId})</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600">
                  {submittedAsMinor
                    ? `Minor profile queued for ${fullName} under parent/legal guardian registration. This profile is marked private/restricted and hidden from all public pages, visible exclusively to verified Clients & Agencies.`
                    : `Your creative profile is queued for ${fullName}. Complete your Club Membership fee via Stripe to unlock instant directory publication and castings.`}
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onClose();
                    onProceedToMembership();
                  }}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
                >
                  Pay Membership Fee ($29/mo)
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-5 space-y-3.5">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Full Name
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Jordan Sinclair"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Email Address
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="jordan@studio.nyc"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                      Discipline
                    </label>
                    <select
                      value={role}
                      onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                    >
                      {ROLE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                        <option key={opt.id} value={opt.label}>
                          {opt.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                      Borough
                    </label>
                    <select
                      value={borough}
                      onChange={(e) => setBorough(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                    >
                      <option value="Manhattan, NY">Manhattan, NY</option>
                      <option value="Brooklyn, NY">Brooklyn, NY</option>
                      <option value="Queens, NY">Queens, NY</option>
                      <option value="Bronx, NY">Bronx, NY</option>
                      <option value="Staten Island, NY">Staten Island, NY</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Instagram or Portfolio URL
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={instagram}
                    onChange={(e) => setInstagram(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="@username or portfolio link"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Required Date of Birth & Automatic Under-18 Privacy Rules */}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Date of Birth *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    required
                    max={todayIso}
                    value={dateOfBirth}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const nextDob = e.target.value;
                      setDateOfBirth(nextDob);
                      const nextAge = calculateExactAge(nextDob);
                      if (nextAge === null || nextAge >= 18) {
                        setGuardianConsent(false);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                  {calculatedAge !== null && (
                    <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-700">
                      {isMinor
                        ? `Calculated Age: ${calculatedAge} · Minor Detected · Visibility: Verified Clients & Agencies Only`
                        : `Calculated Age: ${calculatedAge} · Standard Public Directory Eligibility`}
                    </p>
                  )}
                  <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
                    Applicants under 18 must be registered by a parent or legal guardian/tutor and
                    are automatically restricted from all public Roster &amp; Directory pages.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {isMinor && (
                  <div className="border border-black bg-neutral-50 p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-black leading-snug">
                      Parent / Legal Guardian Registration Required (Under 18)
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                      Profile visibility is locked to verified Clients &amp; Agencies only and hidden
                      from public pages.
                    </p>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Parent / Legal Guardian Full Name *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={guardianName}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Parent or legal guardian full name"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Relationship *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        required
                        value={guardianRelationship}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianRelationship(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="">Select relationship...</option>
                        <option value="Parent">Parent</option>
                        <option value="Legal Guardian">Legal Guardian</option>
                        <option value="Legal Tutor">Legal Tutor</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Parent / Guardian Contact (Email or Phone) *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={guardianContact}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianContact(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="guardian@domain.com or (212) 555-0194"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none pt-1">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        required
                        checked={guardianConsent}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianConsent(e.target.checked)}
                        className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 accent-black shrink-0"
                      />
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-700 leading-snug">
                        I confirm I am the parent or legal guardian/tutor registering this minor and
                        approve private visibility restricted to verified Clients &amp; Agencies. *
                      </span>
                    </label>
                  </div>
                )}

                {errorMsg && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
                    {errorMsg}
                  </p>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
                >
                  {submitting ? 'Submitting...' : 'Submit Club Application'}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="border-t md:border-t-0 md:border-l border-neutral-200 pt-6 md:pt-0 md:pl-8">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
                Active NYC Club Members
              </p>
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500 tabular-nums">
                {filteredMembers.length} active
              </span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={directoryFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setDirectoryFilter(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search models, photographers, stylists..."
              className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none mb-3"
            />
            <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-100 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
              {filteredMembers.map((m) => (
                <div key={m.id} className="py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-black truncate">{m.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{m.role}</p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 shrink-0">{m.location}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface EditorialStoryModalProps {
  story: EditorialStory | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUnlockMembership: () => void;
}

export const EditorialStoryModal: React.FC<EditorialStoryModalProps> = ({
  story,
  onClose,
  onUnlockMembership,
}) => {
  if (!story) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={story.title}
    >
      <div className="bg-white text-black border border-black w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
            {story.kicker} {story.proOnly ? '· Pro Intelligence' : ''}
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close story"
            className="p-1 text-neutral-500 hover:text-black"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="aspect-[16/10] w-full bg-neutral-100 overflow-hidden">
          {story.imageSrc ? (
            <img
              src={story.imageSrc}
              alt={story.title}
              referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
              className="w-full h-full object-cover object-[50%_20%]"
            />
          ) : (
            <EditorialPlate variant={story.variant} className="w-full h-full" label={story.title} />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="p-6">
          <h4 className="font-display font-bold text-xl leading-snug">{story.title}</h4>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider">{story.byline}</p>
          <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed">{story.summary}</p>
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            {story.proOnly && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onClose();
                  onUnlockMembership();
                }}
                className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
              >
                Unlock Full Brief with Membership
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-xs font-semibold hover:border-black transition-colors"
            >
              Close
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface LookbookLightboxProps {
  frame: SocialFrame | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const LookbookLightbox: React.FC<LookbookLightboxProps> = ({ frame, onClose }) => {
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setImageError(false);
  }, [frame?.id, frame?.imageSrc]);

  if (!frame) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={frame.title}
    >
      <div className="bg-white text-black border border-black w-full max-w-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-neutral-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
            NYC Model Club Visual Archive
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close lightbox"
            className="p-1 text-neutral-500 hover:text-black"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="aspect-[4/3] w-full bg-neutral-100 overflow-hidden">
          {frame.imageSrc && !imageError ? (
            <img
              src={frame.imageSrc}
              alt={frame.title}
              referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
              onError={() => setImageError(true)}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top"
            />
          ) : (
            <EditorialPlate variant={frame.plateVariant} className="w-full h-full" label={frame.title} />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h4 className="font-display font-bold text-lg">{frame.title}</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-0.5">{frame.caption}</p>
          </div>
          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">{frame.credit}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface InfoModalProps {
  section: 'about' | 'contact' | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onJumpToManagement: () => void;
}

export const InfoModal: React.FC<InfoModalProps> = ({
  section,
  onClose,
  onJumpToManagement,
}) => {
  if (!section) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="bg-white text-black border border-black w-full max-w-lg p-7 relative">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close dialog"
          className="absolute top-5 right-5 text-neutral-500 hover:text-black"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {section === 'about' ? (
          <>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              Platform &amp; Agency Overview
            </p>
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-2xl mt-2">
              NYC Model Club &amp; New York City Model Management
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed">
              <strong>NYC Model Club</strong> connects emerging and established New York talent
              with verified casting teams, SoHo studio access, and production resources.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed">
              <strong>New York City Model Management</strong> represents a curated roster of
              runway, editorial, commercial, and AI talent, handling direct client inquiries,
              production coordination, and private rate schedules through our booking desk.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  onClose();
                  onJumpToManagement();
                }}
                className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
              >
                View Agency Management
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-xs font-semibold hover:border-black transition-colors"
              >
                Close
              </button>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              Concierge &amp; Booking Desk
            </p>
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-2xl mt-2">Contact &amp; Studio Line</h3>
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 text-sm text-neutral-700">
              <div>
                <span className="block text-xs text-neutral-500">Direct Inquiries</span>
                <a
                  href={`mailto:${CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}`}
                  className="font-medium text-black underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-neutral-600"
                >
                  {CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}
                </a>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span className="block text-xs text-neutral-500">{CONTACT_CONFIG.officeLabel}</span>
                <span className="font-medium text-black">{CONTACT_CONFIG.officeAddress}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="mt-6 w-full py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
            >
              Done
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

```

# src\components\ManagementSection.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowUpRight, X, CheckCircle2, Lock, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  BOOKING_PACKAGES,
  ELITE_ROSTER,
  EliteDivision,
  EliteModel,
  MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES,
} from '../data/clubData';
import { EditorialPlate } from './EditorialPlate';
import type { PaymentProvider } from './MembershipSection';

interface ManagementSectionProps {
  onApplyMembership: () => void;
  stripeConfigured?: boolean;
  squareConfigured?: boolean;
  paymentProvider?: PaymentProvider;
  onChangePaymentProvider?: (provider: PaymentProvider) => void;
  isProd?: boolean;
  verifiedBooking?: { reference: string; modelName: string; provider?: PaymentProvider } | null;
  bookingNotice?: string | null;
  approvedRosterModels?: EliteModel[];
  clientAccessUnlocked?: boolean;
  onVerifyClientLogin?: (email: string, memberCode: string) => Promise<string | null>;
  onLogoutClientAccess?: () => void;
}

type FilterDivision = Exclude<EliteDivision, 'All Roster'>;

const DIVISIONS: FilterDivision[] = [
  'Runway',
  'Editorial',
  'Commercial',
  'AI Representation',
];

export const ManagementSection: React.FC<ManagementSectionProps> = ({
  onApplyMembership,
  stripeConfigured = false,
  squareConfigured = false,
  paymentProvider = 'stripe',
  onChangePaymentProvider,
  isProd: _isProd = false,
  verifiedBooking = null,
  bookingNotice = null,
  approvedRosterModels = [],
  clientAccessUnlocked = false,
  onVerifyClientLogin,
  onLogoutClientAccess,
}) => {
  const [localProvider, setLocalProvider] = useState<PaymentProvider>(paymentProvider);
  const activeProvider = onChangePaymentProvider ? paymentProvider : localProvider;
  const setActiveProvider = (provider: PaymentProvider) => {
    setLocalProvider(provider);
    if (onChangePaymentProvider) {
      onChangePaymentProvider(provider);
    }
  };
  const providerLabel = activeProvider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe';
  const usesHostedCheckout =
    activeProvider === 'square' ? squareConfigured : stripeConfigured;
  const [selectedDivision, setSelectedDivision] = useState<FilterDivision | null>(null);
  const [activeModel, setActiveModel] = useState<EliteModel | null>(null);
  const [failedImages, setFailedImages] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [clientLoginOpen, setClientLoginOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loginEmail, setLoginEmail] = useState('');
  const [loginCode, setLoginCode] = useState('');
  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loginBusy, setLoginBusy] = useState(false);

  // Online Booking & Payment Modal State (20% Client Fee + 20% Model Commission + Optional Media Bundle)
  const [selectedPkgId, setSelectedPkgId] = useState<string>(BOOKING_PACKAGES[1].id);
  const [bundleMedia, setBundleMedia] = useState(false);
  const [selectedMediaId, setSelectedMediaId] = useState<string>(MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES[3].id);
  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState('');
  const [clientEmail, setClientEmail] = useState('');
  const [shootDate, setShootDate] = useState('');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [cardNumber, setCardNumber] = useState('');
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState('');
  const [cvc, setCvc] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [bookingError, setBookingError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [paidReceipt, setPaidReceipt] = useState<{
    reference: string;
    totalClientChargeDollars: number;
    baseRateDollars: number;
    productionRateDollars?: number;
    clientFeeDollars: number;
    memberDiscountDollars?: number;
  } | null>(null);

  const combinedRoster = [...approvedRosterModels, ...ELITE_ROSTER].filter(
    (m) =>
      clientAccessUnlocked ||
      (!m.isMinor &&
        m.visibility !== 'clients_only' &&
        m.visibilityScope !== 'verified_clients_only')
  );
  const visibleModels = selectedDivision
    ? combinedRoster.filter((m) => m.division === selectedDivision)
    : combinedRoster;

  const activePackage =
    BOOKING_PACKAGES.find((p) => p.id === selectedPkgId) || BOOKING_PACKAGES[1];
  const activeMediaService =
    MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.find((s) => s.id === selectedMediaId) ||
    MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES[0];

  const baseRate = activePackage.baseRateDollars;
  const productionRate = bundleMedia ? activeMediaService.rateDollars : 0;
  const clientAgencyFee = Math.round(baseRate * 0.2);
  const grossBookingSubtotal = baseRate + clientAgencyFee + productionRate;
  const memberDiscountDollars = clientAccessUnlocked
    ? Math.round(grossBookingSubtotal * 0.15)
    : 0;
  const totalOnlineCharge = Math.max(0, grossBookingSubtotal - memberDiscountDollars);

  const openCompCard = (model: EliteModel) => {
    setActiveModel(model);
    setSelectedPkgId(BOOKING_PACKAGES[1].id);
    setBundleMedia(false);
    setPaidReceipt(null);
    setBookingError(null);
  };

  const formatCardNumber = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 16);
    return digits.replace(/(\d{4})(?=\d)/g, '$1 ');
  };

  const formatExpiry = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4);
    if (digits.length >= 3) {
      return `${digits.slice(0, 2)}/${digits.slice(2)}`;
    }
    return digits;
  };

  const handleOnlineBookingPayment = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeModel) return;
    setBookingError(null);

    if (!clientName.trim() || !clientEmail.includes('@')) {
      setBookingError('Please enter your brand/agency name and valid email.');
      return;
    }
    if (!usesHostedCheckout) {
      const cleanCard = cardNumber.replace(/\s/g, '');
      if (cleanCard.length < 12 || expiry.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 4 || cvc.length < 3) {
        setBookingError('Please enter valid payment card details to authorize online booking.');
        return;
      }
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/bookings/checkout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          bookingScope: bundleMedia ? 'bundle' : 'model',
          paymentProvider: activeProvider,
          modelId: activeModel.id,
          modelName: activeModel.name,
          packageLabel: activePackage.label,
          productionPackage: bundleMedia ? activeMediaService.title : '',
          baseRateDollars: baseRate,
          productionRateDollars: productionRate,
          clientMember: clientAccessUnlocked,
          clientName: clientName.trim(),
          clientEmail: clientEmail.trim(),
          shootDate: shootDate.trim() || 'Flexible',
          notes: notes.trim(),
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setBookingError(data.error || 'Booking authorization failed.');
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      if ((data.mode === 'stripe_redirect' || data.mode === 'square_redirect') && data.url) {
        window.location.assign(data.url);
        return;
      }

      setPaidReceipt({
        reference: data.reference,
        ...data.breakdown,
      });
      setClientName('');
      setClientEmail('');
      setShootDate('');
      setNotes('');
      setCardNumber('');
      setExpiry('');
      setCvc('');
    } catch {
      setBookingError('Network error authorizing online booking.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section
      id="management"
      className="bg-[#0A0A0A] text-white border-t border-neutral-800 py-12 md:py-16"
    >
      <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
        {/* Top Agency Strip */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-neutral-800">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3">
            <span className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-tight text-white">
              nyc model management.
            </span>
            <span className="font-editorial italic text-base text-neutral-400">
              Private Agency Division
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
              Direct Agency Desk · Rates Upon Inquiry / Client Access
            </span>
            {onVerifyClientLogin && !clientAccessUnlocked && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setLoginError(null);
                  setClientLoginOpen(true);
                }}
                className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-neutral-300 cursor-pointer"
              >
                Client &amp; Agency Login
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Hero Agency Manifesto */}
        <div className="pt-8 pb-9 border-b border-neutral-800 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-end">
          <div className="lg:col-span-8">
            <h2
              className="font-display font-bold tracking-[-0.03em] text-white leading-[1.04]"
              style={{ fontSize: 'clamp(2.1rem, 4.4vw, 3.5rem)', textWrap: 'balance' }}
            >
              Represented Talent &amp; Direct Booking.
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-300 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
              Book curated runway, editorial, commercial, and AI-represented talent through our
              agency desk. Agency rates are shared upon request, with exclusive member discounts for
              Clients &amp; Agencies.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="lg:col-span-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row lg:flex-col items-start lg:items-end gap-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onApplyMembership}
              className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-200 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              Get Scouted via Membership
            </button>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
              Selected directly from active NYC Model Club talent
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Paid Booking Confirmation Banner */}
        {verifiedBooking && (
          <div
            className="mt-8 p-5 border border-white bg-neutral-900 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4"
            role="status"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5 text-white shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
              <div>
                <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
                  {verifiedBooking.provider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe'} Online Booking Confirmed
                </p>
                <p className="font-display font-bold text-base text-white mt-0.5">
                  {verifiedBooking.modelName} booked · Reference {verifiedBooking.reference}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-300 tabular-nums">
              Status: Paid via {verifiedBooking.provider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe'}
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {bookingNotice && !verifiedBooking && (
          <div
            className="mt-8 p-4 border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-900 text-xs text-neutral-300"
            role="status"
          >
            {bookingNotice}
          </div>
        )}

        {clientAccessUnlocked && (
          <div
            className="mt-8 p-4 border border-white/60 bg-neutral-900 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs"
            role="status"
          >
            <span className="font-semibold text-white">
              Clients &amp; Agencies Pass Active: Full Verified Roster, Restricted Profiles &amp; Rate Desk Unlocked
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-300">
                15% Client Member Discount Applied
              </span>
              {onLogoutClientAccess && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onLogoutClientAccess}
                  className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Sign Out
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Division Filter Bar */}
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 p-1 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800">
            {DIVISIONS.map((div) => {
              const active = selectedDivision === div;
              return (
                <button
                  key={div}
                  type="button"
                  aria-pressed={active}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setSelectedDivision((prev) => (prev === div ? null : div))
                  }
                  className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                    active ? 'bg-white text-black' : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  {div}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-400 tabular-nums">
            {visibleModels.length} Represented Talent · Instant Online Booking
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Pioneering AI Model Representation Callout */}
        <div className="mt-6 p-5 border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-neutral-400">
              Pioneering Division · AI Model Representation
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs md:text-sm text-neutral-200 leading-relaxed">
              NYC Model Club is one of{' '}
              <strong className="text-white font-semibold">
                the first New York agencies to represent AI talent
              </strong>
              , offering licensed digital models alongside our main editorial and runway roster.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              setSelectedDivision((prev) =>
                prev === 'AI Representation' ? null : 'AI Representation'
              )
            }
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-neutral-600 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-white hover:border-white transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
          >
            {selectedDivision === 'AI Representation'
              ? 'Show Full Roster'
              : 'View AI Model Roster'}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Represented Models Grid */}
        <div className="mt-10 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-x-8 gap-y-12">
          {visibleModels.map((model) => (
            <article key={model.id} className="group flex flex-col">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => openCompCard(model)}
                className="text-left focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-white"
              >
                <div className="relative aspect-[3/4] overflow-hidden border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950">
                  {model.imageSrc && !failedImages[model.id] ? (
                    <img
                      src={model.imageSrc}
                      alt={`${model.name}, New York City Model Management`}
                      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                      onError={() =>
                        setFailedImages((prev) => ({ ...prev, [model.id]: true }))
                      }
                      className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <EditorialPlate
                      variant={model.plateVariant}
                      darkSurface
                      className="w-full h-full transition-transform duration-300 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.03]"
                      label={`${model.name}, New York City Model Management`}
                    />
                  )}
                  <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 px-2.5 py-1 bg-black/85 border border-neutral-700 text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-white flex items-center gap-1">
                    <span>Book Online</span>
                    <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-4 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 border-b border-neutral-800 pb-2.5">
                  <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl tracking-tight text-white">
                    {model.name}
                  </h3>
                  <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-300 tabular-nums shrink-0">
                    {clientAccessUnlocked
                      ? `$${Math.round(model.dayRateDollars * 0.85).toLocaleString()} / day · Member`
                      : 'Rates on Inquiry'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-400">
                  <span>
                    {model.division} · {model.height} · {model.location}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{model.plateCode}</span>
                </div>

                {model.aiTalent && (
                  <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-neutral-300">
                    AI Model Representation · Digital Campaign Licensing
                  </p>
                )}

                {(model.isMinor ||
                  model.visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                  model.visibilityScope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                  <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-neutral-300">
                    Private Minor Profile · Verified Clients &amp; Agencies Only
                  </p>
                )}

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500 truncate">
                  {model.clients.join(' · ')}
                </p>
              </button>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Comp Card + Online Booking & 20%/20% Commission Payment Modal */}
      {activeModel && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="mgmt-booking-title"
        >
          <div className="bg-[#0c0c0c] text-white border border-neutral-700 w-full max-w-5xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12">
            {/* Left Portrait & Measurements */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-black border-b lg:border-b-0 lg:border-r border-neutral-800 flex flex-col">
              <div className="aspect-[3/4] w-full overflow-hidden bg-neutral-950">
                {activeModel.imageSrc && !failedImages[activeModel.id] ? (
                  <img
                    src={activeModel.imageSrc}
                    alt={`${activeModel.name} comp card`}
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                    onError={() =>
                      setFailedImages((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeModel.id]: true }))
                    }
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover object-top"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <EditorialPlate
                    variant={activeModel.plateVariant}
                    darkSurface
                    className="w-full h-full"
                    label={`${activeModel.name} comp card`}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="p-6 border-t border-neutral-800 space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
                    {activeModel.plateCode} · {activeModel.aiTalent ? 'AI Model Representation' : 'New York City Model Management'}
                  </p>
                  <h3 id="mgmt-booking-title" className="font-display font-bold text-2xl mt-1">
                    {activeModel.name}
                  </h3>
                  {activeModel.licensingTerms && (
                    <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
                      {activeModel.licensingTerms}
                    </p>
                  )}
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-300 leading-relaxed">{activeModel.bio}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <span className="block text-neutral-500">Height</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-white tabular-nums">{activeModel.height}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="block text-neutral-500">Stats</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-white tabular-nums">
                      {activeModel.measurements}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="block text-neutral-500">Shoe</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-white tabular-nums">{activeModel.shoe}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Right: Online Booking & 20% Client + 20% Model Commission Payment */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 p-6 md:p-8 flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-neutral-800">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
                    Direct Online Booking &amp; Payment
                  </p>
                  <h4 className="font-display font-bold text-xl mt-1">
                    Book {activeModel.name} Online
                  </h4>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveModel(null)}
                  aria-label="Close booking dialog"
                  className="p-1.5 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {paidReceipt ? (
                <div className="my-auto py-8">
                  <div className="p-6 border border-neutral-700 bg-neutral-900/70 space-y-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-white font-semibold text-base">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                      <span>Booking Confirmed &amp; Paid ({paidReceipt.reference})</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300 leading-relaxed">
                      New York City Model Management has locked {activeModel.name} for your
                      selected production date. Call sheet and release forms have been sent.
                    </p>
                    <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-800 space-y-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-400">
                        <span>Talent Booking Package</span>
                        <span>${paidReceipt.baseRateDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-400">
                        <span>Agency Booking &amp; Coordination</span>
                        <span>+${paidReceipt.clientFeeDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                      {Boolean(paidReceipt.productionRateDollars) && (
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-400">
                          <span>Media &amp; Visual Works Bundle</span>
                          <span>
                            +${(paidReceipt.productionRateDollars || 0).toLocaleString()}.00
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      {Boolean(paidReceipt.memberDiscountDollars) && (
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-300">
                          <span>Clients &amp; Agencies Member Discount (15%)</span>
                          <span>
                            -${(paidReceipt.memberDiscountDollars || 0).toLocaleString()}.00
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-white font-semibold pt-2 border-t border-neutral-800">
                        <span>Total Paid Online</span>
                        <span>${paidReceipt.totalClientChargeDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <form onSubmit={handleOnlineBookingPayment} className="mt-5 space-y-5">
                  {/* Package Selector */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 mb-2">
                      1. Select Booking Package
                    </label>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                      {BOOKING_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => {
                        const active = pkg.id === selectedPkgId;
                        return (
                          <button
                            key={pkg.id}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setSelectedPkgId(pkg.id)}
                            className={`p-3 text-left border transition-colors ${
                              active
                                ? 'border-white bg-neutral-900 text-white'
                                : 'border-neutral-800 bg-black text-neutral-400 hover:border-neutral-600'
                            }`}
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">{pkg.label}</span>
                              <span className="font-mono text-xs tabular-nums text-white">
                                ${pkg.baseRateDollars.toLocaleString()}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                            <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                              {pkg.duration}
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
```
