```tsx
                  {/* Division Bundling: Model Only vs Bundle with Media & Visual Works */}
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    <span className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400">
                      2. Division Scope (Optional Media Production Bundle)
                    </span>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setBundleMedia(false)}
                        className={`py-2 px-3 text-xs font-semibold border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          !bundleMedia
                            ? 'border-white bg-white text-black'
                            : 'border-neutral-800 bg-black text-neutral-400 hover:border-neutral-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Model Only
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setBundleMedia(true)}
                        className={`py-2 px-3 text-xs font-semibold border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          bundleMedia
                            ? 'border-white bg-white text-black'
                            : 'border-neutral-800 bg-black text-neutral-400 hover:border-neutral-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Bundle + Media Production
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    {bundleMedia && (
                      <select
                        value={selectedMediaId}
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedMediaId(e.target.value)}
                        aria-label="Select Media & Visual Works production package"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.map((svc) => (
                          <option key={svc.id} value={svc.id}>
                            {svc.category}: {svc.title} (+${svc.rateDollars.toLocaleString()})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Client Booking Rate Summary */}
                  <div className="p-4 border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950 space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-300">
                      <span>Talent Package ({activePackage.label})</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">${baseRate.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-300">
                      <span>Agency Booking &amp; Coordination</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                        +${clientAgencyFee.toLocaleString()}.00
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {bundleMedia && (
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-300">
                        <span>Media &amp; Visual Works ({activeMediaService.title})</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                          +${productionRate.toLocaleString()}.00
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {clientAccessUnlocked && memberDiscountDollars > 0 && (
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-200">
                        <span>Clients &amp; Agencies Member Discount (-15%)</span>
                        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                          -${memberDiscountDollars.toLocaleString()}.00
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-white font-semibold text-sm pt-2 border-t border-neutral-800">
                      <span>Total Online Payment Due</span>
                      <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">
                        ${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {!clientAccessUnlocked && (
                      <div className="pt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
                        <span>
                          Clients &amp; Agencies ($99/mo) members save 15% on all talent bookings.
                        </span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setActiveModel(null);
                            onApplyMembership();
                          }}
                          className="text-white underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-neutral-300 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Unlock Member Discount
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Client & Production Info */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={clientName}
                      onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Brand / Casting Director"
                      className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      required
                      value={clientEmail}
                      onChange={(e) => setClientEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="production@brand.com"
                      className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={shootDate}
                      onChange={(e) => setShootDate(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Shoot date(s) e.g. Dec 12, 2026"
                      className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={notes}
                      onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Studio / usage notes"
                      className="px-3 py-2.5 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-500 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Payment Provider Selector */}
                  <div>
                    <span className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 mb-2">
                      3. Payment Method
                    </span>
                    <div
                      className="grid grid-cols-2 border border-neutral-800 bg-black p-0.5"
                      role="group"
                      aria-label="Payment method"
                    >
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        aria-pressed={activeProvider === 'stripe'}
                        onClick={() => setActiveProvider('stripe')}
                        className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          activeProvider === 'stripe'
                            ? 'bg-white text-black'
                            : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Pay with Stripe
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        aria-pressed={activeProvider === 'square'}
                        onClick={() => setActiveProvider('square')}
                        className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                          activeProvider === 'square'
                            ? 'bg-white text-black'
                            : 'text-neutral-400 hover:text-white'
                        }`}
                      >
                        Pay with Square
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Online Card Authorization (Test preview only when Hosted Checkout is not active) */}
                  {!usesHostedCheckout && (
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 mb-2">
                        Online Payment via {providerLabel} (Test Preview)
                      </label>
                      <div className="border border-neutral-700 bg-black focus-within:border-white">
                        <div className="flex items-center px-3.5 py-2.5 border-b border-neutral-800">
                          <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-500 mr-2.5 shrink-0" />
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            value={cardNumber}
                            onChange={(e) => setCardNumber(formatCardNumber(e.target.value))}
                            placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456"
                            className="w-full bg-transparent text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-white placeholder:text-neutral-600 focus:outline-none"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 divide-x divide-neutral-800">
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            value={expiry}
                            onChange={(e) => setExpiry(formatExpiry(e.target.value))}
                            placeholder="MM/YY"
                            className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-transparent text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-white placeholder:text-neutral-600 focus:outline-none"
                          />
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            maxLength={4}
                            value={cvc}
                            onChange={(e) => setCvc(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}
                            placeholder="CVC"
                            className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-transparent text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-white placeholder:text-neutral-600 focus:outline-none"
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {bookingError && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-red-400" role="alert">
                      {bookingError}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submitting}
                    className="w-full py-3.5 bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>
                      {submitting
                        ? usesHostedCheckout
                          ? `Redirecting to ${providerLabel} Checkout...`
                          : `Authorizing via ${providerLabel}...`
                        : usesHostedCheckout
                          ? `Continue to ${providerLabel} Checkout ($${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00)`
                          : `Pay $${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00 via ${providerLabel} & Confirm`}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </form>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Verified Client & Agency Sign-In Modal */}
      {clientLoginOpen && onVerifyClientLogin && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="client-login-title"
        >
          <div className="bg-[#0c0c0c] text-white border border-neutral-700 w-full max-w-md p-7 relative">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setClientLoginOpen(false)}
              aria-label="Close client sign-in"
              className="absolute top-5 right-5 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
              Verified Clients &amp; Agencies Access
            </p>
            <h3 id="client-login-title" className="font-display font-bold text-2xl mt-1">
              Sign In with Client Pass
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
              Enter your active Clients &amp; Agencies membership email and pass code to view
              restricted minor profiles, private roster listings, and 15% member booking rates.
            </p>
            <form
              onSubmit={async (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setLoginError(null);
                setLoginBusy(true);
                const err = await onVerifyClientLogin(loginEmail, loginCode);
                setLoginBusy(false);
                if (err) {
                  setLoginError(err);
                  return;
                }
                setClientLoginOpen(false);
                setLoginEmail('');
                setLoginCode('');
              }}
              className="mt-5 space-y-3.5"
            >
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 mb-1">
                  Client / Agency Email
                </label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={loginEmail}
                  onChange={(e) => setLoginEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="casting@agency.com"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs text-white placeholder:text-neutral-600 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-400 mb-1">
                  Member Pass Code
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={loginCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setLoginCode(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="NYCMC-XXXX"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-black border border-neutral-700 text-xs font-mono uppercase text-white placeholder:text-neutral-600 focus:border-white focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>
              {loginError && (
                <p className="text-xs text-red-400" role="alert">
                  {loginError}
                </p>
              )}
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loginBusy}
                className="w-full py-3 bg-white text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-200 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loginBusy ? 'Verifying Pass...' : 'Unlock Verified Client View'}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
};

```

# src\components\MediaProductionSection.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowRight, Check, CheckCircle2, CreditCard, Lock, X } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  BOOKING_PACKAGES,
  CONTACT_CONFIG,
  ELITE_ROSTER,
  MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES,
  MediaProductionService,
} from '../data/clubData';
import type { PaymentProvider } from './MembershipSection';

interface MediaProductionSectionProps {
  stripeConfigured?: boolean;
  squareConfigured?: boolean;
  paymentProvider?: PaymentProvider;
  onChangePaymentProvider?: (provider: PaymentProvider) => void;
  isProd?: boolean;
  clientAccessUnlocked?: boolean;
  onUnlockClientAccess?: () => void;
}

type BookingScope = 'model' | 'production' | 'bundle';

export const MediaProductionSection: React.FC<MediaProductionSectionProps> = ({
  stripeConfigured = false,
  squareConfigured = false,
  paymentProvider = 'stripe',
  onChangePaymentProvider,
  isProd: _isProd = false,
  clientAccessUnlocked = false,
  onUnlockClientAccess,
}) => {
  const [localProvider, setLocalProvider] = useState<PaymentProvider>(paymentProvider);
  const activeProvider = onChangePaymentProvider ? paymentProvider : localProvider;
  const setActiveProvider = (provider: PaymentProvider) => {
    setLocalProvider(provider);
    if (onChangePaymentProvider) {
      onChangePaymentProvider(provider);
    }
  };
  const providerLabel = activeProvider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe';
  const usesHostedCheckout =
    activeProvider === 'square' ? squareConfigured : stripeConfigured;

  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [bookingScope, setBookingScope] = useState<BookingScope>('production');
  const [selectedModelId, setSelectedModelId] = useState<string>(ELITE_ROSTER[0].id);
  const [selectedModelPkgId, setSelectedModelPkgId] = useState<string>(BOOKING_PACKAGES[1].id);
  const [selectedServiceId, setSelectedServiceId] = useState<string>(
    MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES[3].id
  );

  const [clientName, setClientName] = useState('');
  const [clientEmail, setClientEmail] = useState('');
  const [shootDate, setShootDate] = useState('');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [cardNumber, setCardNumber] = useState('');
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState('');
  const [cvc, setCvc] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [bookingError, setBookingError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [paidReceipt, setPaidReceipt] = useState<{
    reference: string;
    bookingScope: BookingScope;
    baseRateDollars: number;
    productionRateDollars: number;
    clientFeeDollars: number;
    memberDiscountDollars?: number;
    totalClientChargeDollars: number;
  } | null>(null);

  const selectedModel =
    ELITE_ROSTER.find((m) => m.id === selectedModelId) || ELITE_ROSTER[0];
  const selectedModelPackage =
    BOOKING_PACKAGES.find((p) => p.id === selectedModelPkgId) || BOOKING_PACKAGES[1];
  const selectedService =
    MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.find((s) => s.id === selectedServiceId) ||
    MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES[0];

  const includesModel = bookingScope === 'model' || bookingScope === 'bundle';
  const includesProduction = bookingScope === 'production' || bookingScope === 'bundle';

  const modelRate = includesModel ? selectedModelPackage.baseRateDollars : 0;
  const productionRate = includesProduction ? selectedService.rateDollars : 0;
  const clientAgencyFee = includesModel ? Math.round(modelRate * 0.2) : 0;
  const grossBookingSubtotal = modelRate + clientAgencyFee + productionRate;
  const memberDiscountDollars = clientAccessUnlocked
    ? Math.round(grossBookingSubtotal * 0.15)
    : 0;
  const totalOnlineCharge = Math.max(0, grossBookingSubtotal - memberDiscountDollars);

  const openBookingDesk = (
    scope: BookingScope,
    service?: MediaProductionService
  ) => {
    setBookingScope(scope);
    if (service) {
      setSelectedServiceId(service.id);
    }
    setPaidReceipt(null);
    setBookingError(null);
    setModalOpen(true);
  };

  const formatCardNumber = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 16);
    return digits.replace(/(\d{4})(?=\d)/g, '$1 ');
  };

  const formatExpiry = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4);
    if (digits.length >= 3) {
      return `${digits.slice(0, 2)}/${digits.slice(2)}`;
    }
    return digits;
  };

  const handleSubmitBooking = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBookingError(null);

    if (!clientName.trim() || !clientEmail.includes('@')) {
      setBookingError('Please enter your brand/agency name and a valid business email.');
      return;
    }

    if (!usesHostedCheckout) {
      const cleanCard = cardNumber.replace(/\s/g, '');
      if (cleanCard.length < 12 || expiry.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 4 || cvc.length < 3) {
        setBookingError('Please enter valid payment card details to authorize online booking.');
        return;
      }
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/bookings/checkout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          bookingScope,
          paymentProvider: activeProvider,
          modelId: includesModel ? selectedModel.id : 'media-only',
          modelName: includesModel ? selectedModel.name : 'Media & Visual Works Only',
          packageLabel: includesModel
            ? selectedModelPackage.label
            : selectedService.title,
          productionPackage: includesProduction ? selectedService.title : '',
          baseRateDollars: modelRate,
          productionRateDollars: productionRate,
          clientMember: clientAccessUnlocked,
          clientName: clientName.trim(),
          clientEmail: clientEmail.trim(),
          shootDate: shootDate.trim() || 'Flexible',
          notes: notes.trim(),
        }),
      });
      const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        mode?: string;
        url?: string;
        reference?: string;
        error?: string;
        breakdown?: {
          bookingScope: BookingScope;
          baseRateDollars: number;
          productionRateDollars: number;
          clientFeeDollars: number;
          memberDiscountDollars?: number;
          totalClientChargeDollars: number;
        };
      };

      if (!res.ok) {
        setBookingError(data.error || 'Booking authorization failed.');
        return;
      }

      if ((data.mode === 'stripe_redirect' || data.mode === 'square_redirect') && data.url) {
        window.location.assign(data.url);
        return;
      }

      if (data.breakdown) {
        setPaidReceipt({
          reference: data.reference || 'NYCMM',
          ...data.breakdown,
        });
      }
      setClientName('');
      setClientEmail('');
      setShootDate('');
      setNotes('');
      setCardNumber('');
      setExpiry('');
      setCvc('');
    } catch {
      setBookingError('Network error authorizing production booking.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section
      id="media-production"
      className="bg-white text-black border-t border-neutral-200 py-12 md:py-16"
    >
      <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
        {/* Editorial Division Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-end justify-between gap-6 pb-8 border-b border-neutral-200">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-neutral-500 mb-1.5">
              Division 02 · Media &amp; Visual Works
            </p>
            <h2
              className="font-editorial italic font-normal tracking-[-0.02em] text-black leading-[1.03]"
              style={{ fontSize: 'clamp(2.1rem, 4vw, 3.25rem)' }}
            >
              Media &amp; Visual Works.
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-2.5 text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed max-w-xl">
              Photography, film &amp; motion, art direction, and lookbook production, booked
              standalone as <strong className="font-semibold text-black">Media Only</strong> or{' '}
              <strong className="font-semibold text-black">bundled with represented models</strong>.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => openBookingDesk('bundle')}
              className="px-5 py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              Bundle Models + Media
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => openBookingDesk('production')}
              className="px-5 py-3 border border-black text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              Book Media Only
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Services Bar */}
        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <span className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-medium tracking-wide whitespace-nowrap">
            All Services
          </span>
          <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500 tabular-nums">
            {clientAccessUnlocked
              ? `${MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.length} Units · 15% Client Member Discount Active`
              : 'Rates available via Booking Desk · 15% Off for Client Members'}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* 2x2 Architectural Grid */}
        <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 border border-neutral-200 divide-y md:divide-y-0 md:grid-flow-row">
          {MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.map((service, index) => (
            <article
              key={service.id}
              className={`p-7 md:p-9 flex flex-col justify-between bg-white ${
                index % 2 === 0 ? 'md:border-r md:border-neutral-200' : ''
              } ${index >= 2 ? 'md:border-t md:border-neutral-200' : ''}`}
            >
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4 border-b border-neutral-200 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-neutral-500">
                  <span>
                    {service.indexLabel} · {service.category}
                  </span>
                  <span>{service.turnaround}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
                  <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg xl:text-[19px] tracking-[-0.025em] leading-snug text-black min-w-0">
                    {service.title}
                  </h3>
                  {clientAccessUnlocked && (
                    <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.08em] font-semibold text-neutral-700 tabular-nums shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap">
                      ${Math.round(service.rateDollars * 0.85).toLocaleString()} · Member
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {service.exampleNote && (
                  <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-600">
                    {service.exampleNote}
                  </p>
                )}

                <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">{service.summary}</p>

                <ul className="mt-5 space-y-2 text-xs text-neutral-700">
                  {service.deliverables.map((item) => {
                    const colonIdx = item.indexOf(':');
                    const hasPrefix = colonIdx > 0 && colonIdx < 22;
                    return (
                      <li key={item} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black shrink-0 mt-0.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                        <span>
                          {hasPrefix ? (
                            <>
                              <strong className="font-semibold text-black">
                                {item.slice(0, colonIdx + 1)}
                              </strong>
                              {item.slice(colonIdx + 1)}
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            item
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </li>
                    );
                  })}
                </ul>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-8 pt-5 border-t border-neutral-200 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openBookingDesk('production', service)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <span>Book Production Only</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => openBookingDesk('bundle', service)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 border border-black text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Bundle with Model
                </button>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Custom Projects / Custom Quote Option */}
        <div className="border-x border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-7 md:p-9 flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-neutral-500">
              05 · Bespoke Production Scope
            </p>
            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-4 gap-y-1">
              <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-xl md:text-2xl tracking-tight text-black">
                Bigger Scale? Let&rsquo;s Build It
              </h3>
              <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-neutral-700 shrink-0">
                Custom Quote
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
              Launching 25+ looks, booking multiple shoot days, or pairing models with full media
              production? Reach out and we&rsquo;ll price a custom package for your drop.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
              Custom shot lists · Multi-day bookings · Full usage options · Dedicated edit schedule
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
            <a
              href={`mailto:${CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(
                'Custom Media & Visual Works Quote | NYC Model Club'
              )}`}
              className="px-5 py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              <span>Request Custom Quote</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            </a>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => openBookingDesk('bundle')}
              className="px-5 py-3 border border-black text-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              Configure Custom Bundle
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Unified Booking Modal: Models Only | Media Production Only | Bundle Models + Media */}
      {modalOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-labelledby="media-booking-title"
        >
          <div className="bg-white text-black border border-black w-full max-w-3xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto shadow-2xl">
            <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-black text-white flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <Lock className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                  NYC Model Club · Production &amp; Roster Booking Desk
                </span>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)}
                aria-label="Close booking desk"
                className="p-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {paidReceipt ? (
              <div className="p-8 md:p-10 space-y-5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-black font-semibold text-lg">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  <span>Booking Confirmed ({paidReceipt.reference})</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                  {paidReceipt.bookingScope === 'production'
                    ? `Media & Visual Works package "${selectedService.title}" is scheduled.`
                    : paidReceipt.bookingScope === 'bundle'
                      ? `Integrated bundle (${selectedModel.name} + ${selectedService.title}) is scheduled.`
                      : `Model booking for ${selectedModel.name} is confirmed.`}
                </p>

                <div className="p-4 border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 space-y-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                  {paidReceipt.baseRateDollars > 0 && (
                    <>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-600">
                        <span>Talent Booking Package</span>
                        <span>${paidReceipt.baseRateDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-600">
                        <span>Agency Booking &amp; Coordination</span>
                        <span>+${paidReceipt.clientFeeDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}
                  {paidReceipt.productionRateDollars > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-600">
                      <span>Media &amp; Visual Works Production</span>
                      <span>${paidReceipt.productionRateDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {Boolean(paidReceipt.memberDiscountDollars) && (
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-black font-medium">
                      <span>Clients &amp; Agencies Member Discount (15%)</span>
                      <span>-${(paidReceipt.memberDiscountDollars || 0).toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-black font-semibold pt-2 border-t border-neutral-200">
                    <span>Total Paid Online</span>
                    <span>${paidReceipt.totalClientChargeDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-6 py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
                >
                  Done
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmitBooking} className="p-6 md:p-8 space-y-5">
                <div>
                  <h3 id="media-booking-title" className="font-display font-bold text-2xl">
                    Configure Your Booking
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
                    Choose whether to book represented models independently, Media &amp; Visual
                    Works independently, or both divisions together in one production checkout.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {/* 3-Way Scope Switcher */}
                <div>
                  <span className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-600 mb-2">
                    1. Booking Scope
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    {(
                      [
                        { id: 'model', label: 'Models Only' },
                        { id: 'production', label: 'Media Production Only' },
                        { id: 'bundle', label: 'Bundle: Models + Media' },
                      ] as const
                    ).map((opt) => {
                      const active = bookingScope === opt.id;
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={opt.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setBookingScope(opt.id)}
                          className={`py-2.5 px-3 text-xs font-semibold border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                            active
                              ? 'bg-black text-white border-black'
                              : 'bg-white text-neutral-700 border-neutral-300 hover:border-black'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {opt.label}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Model Selection */}
                {includesModel && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-neutral-200">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Represented Model
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={selectedModelId}
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedModelId(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {ELITE_ROSTER.map((m) => (
                          <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                            {m.name} ({m.division})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Model Booking Package
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={selectedModelPkgId}
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedModelPkgId(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        {BOOKING_PACKAGES.map((pkg) => (
                          <option key={pkg.id} value={pkg.id}>
                            {pkg.label} (${pkg.baseRateDollars.toLocaleString()})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Media Production Selection */}
                {includesProduction && (
                  <div className="pt-2 border-t border-neutral-200">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                      Media &amp; Visual Works Package
                    </label>
                    <select
                      value={selectedServiceId}
                      onChange={(e) => setSelectedServiceId(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                    >
                      {MEDIA_PRODUCTION_SERVICES.map((svc) => (
                        <option key={svc.id} value={svc.id}>
                          {svc.category}: {svc.title} (${svc.rateDollars.toLocaleString()})
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Breakdown Summary */}
                <div className="p-4 border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 space-y-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                  {includesModel && (
                    <>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-700">
                        <span>
                          Talent Package ({selectedModel.name} · {selectedModelPackage.label})
                        </span>
                        <span>${modelRate.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-700">
                        <span>Agency Booking &amp; Coordination</span>
                        <span>+${clientAgencyFee.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}
                  {includesProduction && (
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-neutral-700">
                      <span>Media Production ({selectedService.title})</span>
                      <span>${productionRate.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {clientAccessUnlocked && memberDiscountDollars > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-black font-medium">
                      <span>Clients &amp; Agencies Member Discount (-15%)</span>
                      <span>-${memberDiscountDollars.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-black font-semibold text-sm pt-2 border-t border-neutral-300">
                    <span>Total Online Checkout Due</span>
                    <span>${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00</span>
                  </div>
                  {!clientAccessUnlocked && (
                    <div className="pt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] font-sans text-neutral-600">
                      <span>
                        Clients &amp; Agencies ($99/mo) members save 15% on talent &amp; media
                        bookings.
                      </span>
                      {onUnlockClientAccess && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setModalOpen(false);
                            onUnlockClientAccess();
                          }}
                          className="font-semibold text-black underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-neutral-600 cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Unlock Member Discount
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Client Details */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={clientName}
                    onChange={(e) => setClientName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Brand / Agency Name"
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={clientEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setClientEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="production@brand.com"
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={shootDate}
                    onChange={(e) => setShootDate(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Preferred shoot date(s)"
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={notes}
                    onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Creative brief / shoot notes"
                    className="px-3 py-2 text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-600 mb-2">
                    2. Payment Method
                  </span>
                  <div
                    className="grid grid-cols-2 border border-neutral-300 bg-neutral-100 p-0.5"
                    role="group"
                    aria-label="Payment method"
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-pressed={activeProvider === 'stripe'}
                      onClick={() => setActiveProvider('stripe')}
                      className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                        activeProvider === 'stripe'
                          ? 'bg-black text-white'
                          : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                      }`}
                    >
                      Pay with Stripe
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-pressed={activeProvider === 'square'}
                      onClick={() => setActiveProvider('square')}
                      className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                        activeProvider === 'square'
                          ? 'bg-black text-white'
                          : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                      }`}
                    >
                      Pay with Square
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {!usesHostedCheckout && (
                  <div className="border border-neutral-300 focus-within:border-black">
                    <div className="flex items-center px-3.5 py-2.5 border-b border-neutral-200">
                      <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 mr-2.5 shrink-0" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        value={cardNumber}
                        onChange={(e) => setCardNumber(formatCardNumber(e.target.value))}
                        placeholder={`Card number (${providerLabel} test preview)`}
                        className="w-full text-xs font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 divide-x divide-neutral-200">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        value={expiry}
                        onChange={(e) => setExpiry(formatExpiry(e.target.value))}
                        placeholder="MM/YY"
                        className="px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                      />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        maxLength={4}
                        value={cvc}
                        onChange={(e) => setCvc(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}
                        placeholder="CVC"
                        className="px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {bookingError && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
                    {bookingError}
                  </p>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="w-full py-3.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {submitting
                    ? usesHostedCheckout
                      ? `Redirecting to ${providerLabel} Checkout...`
                      : `Authorizing via ${providerLabel}...`
                    : usesHostedCheckout
                      ? `Continue to ${providerLabel} ($${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00)`
                      : `Confirm & Pay $${totalOnlineCharge.toLocaleString()}.00 via ${providerLabel}`}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
};
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Lock, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { MEMBERSHIP_TIERS, MembershipTier } from '../data/clubData';

export type PaymentProvider = 'stripe' | 'square';

interface MembershipSectionProps {
  onSelectStripePlan: (
    tier: MembershipTier,
    billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual',
    paymentProvider?: PaymentProvider
  ) => void;
  paymentProvider?: PaymentProvider;
  onChangePaymentProvider?: (provider: PaymentProvider) => void;
  activeMemberBadge: { planName: string; memberCode: string } | null;
  loadingTierId?: string | null;
  checkoutNotice?: { tone: 'success' | 'info' | 'error'; message: string } | null;
  checkoutError?: string | null;
  errorTierId?: string | null;
  onOpenOnboarding?: () => void;
}

export const MembershipSection: React.FC<MembershipSectionProps> = ({
  onSelectStripePlan,
  activeMemberBadge,
  loadingTierId = null,
  checkoutNotice = null,
  checkoutError = null,
  errorTierId = null,
  onOpenOnboarding,
}) => {
  const [billingCycle, setBillingCycle] = useState<'monthly' | 'annual'>('monthly');

  return (
    <section
      id="membership"
      className="bg-white text-black border-t border-neutral-200 py-12 md:py-16"
    >
      <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10">
        {/* Editorial Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-end justify-between gap-6 pb-8 border-b border-neutral-200">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] uppercase text-neutral-500 mb-1.5">
              Club Access &amp; Membership
            </p>
            <h2
              className="font-editorial font-normal italic tracking-[-0.02em] text-black leading-[1.02]"
              style={{ fontSize: 'clamp(2.1rem, 4vw, 3.25rem)' }}
            >
              Membership &amp; Access.
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-2.5 text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed max-w-xl">
              Three access tiers for talent, studio members, and casting clients with instant
              activation.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col items-start lg:items-end gap-2 shrink-0">
            {/* Billing Interval Toggle */}
            <div
              className="inline-flex items-center p-1 border border-neutral-300 bg-neutral-100"
              role="group"
              aria-label="Billing interval"
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setBillingCycle('monthly')}
                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                  billingCycle === 'monthly'
                    ? 'bg-black text-white'
                    : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                }`}
              >
                Monthly
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setBillingCycle('annual')}
                className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
                  billingCycle === 'annual'
                    ? 'bg-black text-white'
                    : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                }`}
              >
                Annual (Save 20%)
              </button>
            </div>

            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
              {billingCycle === 'annual'
                ? 'Billed annually · Cancel anytime'
                : 'Billed monthly · Cancel anytime'}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Active Confirmation / Checkout Notice Banner */}
        {activeMemberBadge && (
          <div className="mt-6 p-5 border border-black bg-neutral-50 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
                Active Access Confirmed
              </p>
              <p className="font-display font-bold text-base text-black mt-0.5">
                {activeMemberBadge.planName} · Pass {activeMemberBadge.memberCode}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-700 tabular-nums">
                Status: Active Member
              </span>
              {onOpenOnboarding && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onOpenOnboarding}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Open Onboarding Desk
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {(checkoutError || (checkoutNotice && (!activeMemberBadge || checkoutNotice.tone === 'error'))) && (
          <div
            className={`mt-6 p-4 border text-xs font-medium ${
              checkoutError || checkoutNotice?.tone === 'error'
                ? 'border-red-600 text-red-700 bg-red-50'
                : 'border-neutral-300 text-neutral-700 bg-neutral-50'
            }`}
            role={checkoutError || checkoutNotice?.tone === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'}
          >
            {checkoutError || checkoutNotice?.message}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 3-Column Architectural Editorial Grid */}
        <div className="mt-10 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 border border-neutral-200 divide-y lg:divide-y-0 lg:divide-x divide-neutral-200">
          {MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.map((tier) => {
            const displayPrice =
              billingCycle === 'annual' ? tier.annualMonthlyPrice : tier.monthlyPrice;
            const isHighlighted = Boolean(tier.highlighted);

            return (
              <article
                key={tier.id}
                className={`p-7 xl:p-9 flex flex-col justify-between ${
                  isHighlighted ? 'bg-[#0A0A0A] text-white' : 'bg-white text-black'
                }`}
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4 border-b border-current/15 mb-6">
                    <span
                      className={`font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] uppercase ${
                        isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-400' : 'text-neutral-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {tier.indexLabel} · {tier.kicker}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="font-editorial font-normal italic text-2xl xl:text-[1.75rem] leading-tight">
                    {tier.name}
                  </h3>

                  <div className="mt-5 flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                    <span className="font-display font-bold text-4xl xl:text-[2.75rem] tracking-tight tabular-nums">
                      ${displayPrice}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-mono ${
                        isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-400' : 'text-neutral-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      / month
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {billingCycle === 'annual' ? (
                    <p
                      className={`mt-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] tabular-nums ${
                        isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-400' : 'text-neutral-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      ${tier.annualMonthlyPrice * 12} billed annually
                    </p>
                  ) : (
                    <p
                      className={`mt-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] ${
                        isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-400' : 'text-neutral-500'
                      }`}
                    >
                      Monthly recurring · Instant activation
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <p
                    className={`mt-5 text-sm leading-relaxed ${
                      isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-300' : 'text-neutral-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tier.description}
                  </p>

                  <ul className="mt-6 space-y-2.5 text-[13px] leading-snug">
                    {tier.features.map((feat) => (
                      <li key={feat} className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                        <Check
                          className={`w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0 ${
                            isHighlighted ? 'text-white' : 'text-black'
                          }`}
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                        <span className={isHighlighted ? 'text-neutral-200' : 'text-neutral-700'}>
                          {feat}
                        </span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-current/15">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={loadingTierId !== null}
                    aria-busy={loadingTierId === tier.id}
                    onClick={() => onSelectStripePlan(tier, billingCycle, 'stripe')}
                    className={`w-full py-3.5 px-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-60 cursor-pointer ${
                      isHighlighted
                        ? 'bg-white text-black hover:bg-neutral-200'
                        : 'bg-black text-white hover:bg-neutral-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>
                      {loadingTierId === tier.id ? 'Starting Checkout...' : tier.ctaLabel}
                    </span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </button>
                  {checkoutError && errorTierId === tier.id && (
                    <p
                      className={`mt-3 text-xs font-medium ${
                        isHighlighted ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-red-600'
                      }`}
                      role="alert"
                    >
                      {checkoutError}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-neutral-500">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>
              Encrypted membership checkout · Multiple payment options available at checkout
            </span>
          </div>
          <span>Model &amp; Talent · Studio Access · Clients &amp; Agencies</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};
```

# src\components\OnboardingPage.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Calendar,
  Camera,
  CheckCircle2,
  MailCheck,
  ShieldCheck,
  Upload,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  CONTACT_CONFIG,
  MEMBERSHIP_TIERS,
  MembershipPlanId,
  calculateExactAge,
} from '../data/clubData';

interface OnboardingRecordPayload {
  id: string;
  plan_id: MembershipPlanId;
  billing_cycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  stage_name: string;
  measurements: string;
  location: string;
  portfolio_url: string;
  digitals_url: string;
  studio_shoot_slot: string;
  company_name: string;
  company_role: string;
  project_type: string;
  date_of_birth?: string;
  is_minor?: number;
  guardian_name?: string;
  guardian_relationship?: string;
  guardian_contact?: string;
  guardian_consent?: number;
  profile_visibility?: 'public' | 'clients_only';
  visibility_scope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  review_status: string;
  confirmation_notified_at: string | null;
  confirmation_channel: string;
}

interface VerifiedSessionInfo {
  confirmed: boolean;
  planId: MembershipPlanId;
  planName: string;
  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  amountCents: number;
  paymentStatus: string;
  memberCode: string;
  email: string;
  fullName: string;
  dateOfBirth?: string;
  isMinor?: boolean;
  guardianName?: string;
  guardianRelationship?: string;
  guardianContact?: string;
  guardianConsent?: boolean;
  visibilityScope?: 'public' | 'verified_clients_only';
  onboarding?: OnboardingRecordPayload | null;
}

interface OnboardingPageProps {
  onNavigateHome: (target?: { sectionId?: string; workCategory?: 'Digitals'; clientUnlocked?: boolean }) => void;
  onStartMembershipCheckout: (planId: MembershipPlanId) => void;
  onVerifiedMemberBadge: (badge: { planName: string; memberCode: string; planId: MembershipPlanId }) => void;
}

const STUDIO_TIME_WINDOWS = [
  'Morning · 10:00 AM to 12:30 PM',
  'Afternoon · 1:30 PM to 4:00 PM',
  'Late Afternoon · 4:30 PM to 6:30 PM',
];

const CLIENT_ROLES = ['Casting Director', 'Brand', 'Agency'] as const;

const CLIENT_PROJECT_TYPES = [
  'Runway / Presentation',
  'Editorial / Campaign',
  'Lookbook / E-Commerce Catalog',
  'AI Model Licensing',
  'Models + Media Production Bundle',
];

async function compressImageFileToDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Unable to read image file.'));
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Invalid image format. Please upload JPG, PNG, or WebP.'));
      img.onload = () => {
        const maxDim = 960;
        const scale = Math.min(1, maxDim / Math.max(img.width, img.height));
        const targetW = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width * scale));
        const targetH = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.height * scale));
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = targetW;
        canvas.height = targetH;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          reject(new Error('Browser canvas unavailable for image processing.'));
          return;
        }
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, targetW, targetH);
        const compressed = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82);
        resolve(compressed);
      };
      img.src = String(reader.result || '');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

export const OnboardingPage: React.FC<OnboardingPageProps> = ({
  onNavigateHome,
  onStartMembershipCheckout,
  onVerifiedMemberBadge,
}) => {
  const [verifying, setVerifying] = useState(true);
  const [verifyingMessage, setVerifyingMessage] = useState(
    'Confirming your membership activation...'
  );
  const [verifyError, setVerifyError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sessionInfo, setSessionInfo] = useState<VerifiedSessionInfo | null>(null);
  const [sessionId, setSessionId] = useState<string>('');

  // Talent / Studio form state
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [stageName, setStageName] = useState('');
  const [measurements, setMeasurements] = useState('');
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('');
  const [portfolioUrl, setPortfolioUrl] = useState('');
  const [digitalsUrl, setDigitalsUrl] = useState('');
  const [digitalsFileName, setDigitalsFileName] = useState('');
  const [uploadBusy, setUploadBusy] = useState(false);

  // $59 Studio Access SoHo / Crosby Street booking preference
  const [studioDate, setStudioDate] = useState('');
  const [studioWindow, setStudioWindow] = useState(STUDIO_TIME_WINDOWS[0]);
  const [studioNotes, setStudioNotes] = useState('');

  // $99 Client & Agency form state
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState('');
  const [companyRole, setCompanyRole] = useState<string>(CLIENT_ROLES[0]);
  const [projectType, setProjectType] = useState(CLIENT_PROJECT_TYPES[0]);

  // Minor / parent-guardian registration state (driven automatically by Date of Birth)
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState('');
  const [guardianName, setGuardianName] = useState('');
  const [guardianRelationship, setGuardianRelationship] = useState('');
  const [guardianContact, setGuardianContact] = useState('');
  const [guardianConsent, setGuardianConsent] = useState(false);

  const calculatedAge = calculateExactAge(dateOfBirth);
  const isMinor = calculatedAge !== null && calculatedAge < 18;
  const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  // Submission & confirmation notification state
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [savedRecord, setSavedRecord] = useState<OnboardingRecordPayload | null>(null);
  const [notificationMessage, setNotificationMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const paramSessionId =
      params.get('session_id')?.trim() || sessionStorage.getItem('nycmc_last_session_id') || '';
    const paramPlanRaw = params.get('plan');
    const paramPlan: MembershipPlanId =
      paramPlanRaw === 'studio' || paramPlanRaw === 'clients' ? paramPlanRaw : 'talent';
    const paramCycle = params.get('cycle') === 'annual' ? 'annual' : 'monthly';

    if (!paramSessionId) {
      setVerifying(false);
      setVerifyError(
        'No active Stripe checkout session was detected. Complete checkout on any membership tier to unlock onboarding.'
      );
      return;
    }

    setSessionId(paramSessionId);
    sessionStorage.setItem('nycmc_last_session_id', paramSessionId);

    const hydrateFromRecord = (info: VerifiedSessionInfo) => {
      setSessionInfo(info);
      setEmail(info.email || '');
      setFullName(info.fullName || '');
      onVerifiedMemberBadge({
        planName: info.planName,
        memberCode: info.memberCode,
        planId: info.planId,
      });

      if (info.onboarding) {
        const ob = info.onboarding;
        setSavedRecord(ob);
        setEmail(ob.email || info.email || '');
        setFullName(ob.full_name || info.fullName || '');
        setStageName(ob.stage_name || '');
        setMeasurements(ob.measurements || '');
        setLocation(ob.location || '');
        setPortfolioUrl(ob.portfolio_url || '');
        setDigitalsUrl(ob.digitals_url || '');
        setCompanyName(ob.company_name || '');
        if (ob.company_role) setCompanyRole(ob.company_role);
        if (ob.project_type) setProjectType(ob.project_type);
        setDateOfBirth(ob.date_of_birth || info.dateOfBirth || '');
        setGuardianName(ob.guardian_name || info.guardianName || '');
        setGuardianRelationship(ob.guardian_relationship || info.guardianRelationship || '');
        setGuardianContact(ob.guardian_contact || info.guardianContact || '');
        setGuardianConsent(ob.guardian_consent === 1 || Boolean(info.guardianConsent));
        if (ob.studio_shoot_slot) {
          const [datePart, ...rest] = ob.studio_shoot_slot.split(' · ');
          if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(datePart)) {
            setStudioDate(datePart);
            if (rest.length > 0) setStudioNotes(rest.join(' · '));
          } else {
            setStudioNotes(ob.studio_shoot_slot);
          }
        }
      } else if (info.dateOfBirth) {
        setDateOfBirth(info.dateOfBirth);
        setGuardianName(info.guardianName || '');
        setGuardianRelationship(info.guardianRelationship || '');
        setGuardianContact(info.guardianContact || '');
        setGuardianConsent(Boolean(info.guardianConsent));
      }
    };

    let cancelled = false;
    let pollTimer: number | undefined;
    let attempts = 0;
    const maxWebhookPollAttempts = 15;

    const verifyCheckoutSession = async () => {
      attempts += 1;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/checkout-session/${encodeURIComponent(paramSessionId)}`);
        const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as Partial<VerifiedSessionInfo> & {
          pendingWebhook?: boolean;
          message?: string;
          error?: string;
        };

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (data.pendingWebhook) {
          setVerifyingMessage(
            data.message ||
              'Awaiting verified Stripe checkout.session.completed webhook confirmation...'
          );
          if (attempts < maxWebhookPollAttempts) {
            pollTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
              void verifyCheckoutSession();
            }, 1500);
            return;
          }
          setVerifyError(
            'Stripe payment confirmation webhook has not arrived yet. Please refresh this page in a few seconds.'
          );
          setVerifying(false);
          return;
        }

        if (!res.ok || !data.confirmed) {
          if (res.status === 403 || res.status === 404) {
            sessionStorage.removeItem('nycmc_last_session_id');
          }
          setVerifyError(
            data.error ||
              'Payment has not been verified by Stripe checkout.session.completed.'
          );
          setVerifying(false);
          return;
        }

        const resolvedPlanId: MembershipPlanId =
          data.planId === 'studio' || data.planId === 'clients' || data.planId === 'talent'
            ? data.planId
            : paramPlan;
        const tierObj =
          MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.find((t) => t.id === resolvedPlanId) || MEMBERSHIP_TIERS[0];

        hydrateFromRecord({
          confirmed: true,
          planId: resolvedPlanId,
          planName: data.planName || `NYC Model Club: ${tierObj.name}`,
          billingCycle: data.billingCycle === 'annual' ? 'annual' : paramCycle,
          amountCents:
            typeof data.amountCents === 'number'
              ? data.amountCents
              : paramCycle === 'annual'
                ? tierObj.annualMonthlyPrice * 12 * 100
                : tierObj.monthlyPrice * 100,
          paymentStatus: data.paymentStatus || 'Paid · Active',
          memberCode: data.memberCode || paramSessionId.slice(-6).toUpperCase(),
          email: data.email || '',
          fullName: data.fullName || '',
          dateOfBirth: data.dateOfBirth || '',
          isMinor: Boolean(data.isMinor),
          guardianName: data.guardianName || '',
          guardianRelationship: data.guardianRelationship || '',
          guardianContact: data.guardianContact || '',
          guardianConsent: Boolean(data.guardianConsent),
          visibilityScope: data.visibilityScope,
          onboarding: data.onboarding || null,
        });
        setVerifying(false);
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setVerifyError('Network error verifying Stripe Checkout session.');
          setVerifying(false);
        }
      }
    };

    void verifyCheckoutSession();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (pollTimer !== undefined) {
        window.clearTimeout(pollTimer);
      }
    };
  }, [onVerifiedMemberBadge]);

  const handleDigitalsUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setFormError(null);

    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setFormError('Please select a JPG, PNG, or WebP headshot/digital image.');
      return;
    }
    if (file.size > 12 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setFormError('Image file must be under 12MB.');
      return;
    }

    setUploadBusy(true);
    try {
      const compressed = await compressImageFileToDataUrl(file);
      setDigitalsUrl(compressed);
      setDigitalsFileName(file.name);
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to process uploaded headshot.');
    } finally {
      setUploadBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitOnboarding = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!sessionInfo) return;
    setFormError(null);
    setNotificationMessage(null);

    const planId = sessionInfo.planId;
    if (!email.trim() || !email.includes('@')) {
      setFormError('Please enter a valid email address for your membership confirmation.');
      return;
    }

    let combinedShootSlot = '';
    if (planId === 'studio') {
      if (!studioDate) {
        setFormError('Please choose your preferred date for the monthly SoHo / Crosby Street shoot.');
        return;
      }
      combinedShootSlot = [studioDate, studioWindow, studioNotes.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    }

    if (planId === 'clients') {
      if (companyName.trim().length < 2) {
        setFormError('Please enter your company or brand name.');
        return;
      }
    } else {
      if (fullName.trim().length < 2 || measurements.trim().length < 2 || location.trim().length < 2) {
        setFormError('Please complete your full name, height/measurements, and location.');
        return;
      }
      if (calculatedAge === null) {
        setFormError('Please enter a valid Date of Birth.');
        return;
      }
      if (isMinor) {
        if (guardianName.trim().length < 2) {
          setFormError(
            'Applicants under 18 (minors) must be registered by a parent or legal guardian/tutor. Please enter their full name.'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (!guardianRelationship) {
          setFormError(
            'Please select the guardian relationship (Parent, Legal Guardian, or Legal Tutor).'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (guardianContact.trim().length < 4) {
          setFormError(
            'Please provide parent or legal guardian/tutor contact details (email or phone).'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (!guardianConsent) {
          setFormError(
            'Parent or legal guardian/tutor confirmation is required to register an applicant under 18.'
          );
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/onboarding', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          sessionId,
          planId,
          billingCycle: sessionInfo.billingCycle,
          email: email.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          stageName: stageName.trim(),
          measurements: measurements.trim(),
          location: location.trim(),
          portfolioUrl: portfolioUrl.trim(),
          digitalsUrl: digitalsUrl.trim(),
          studioShootSlot: combinedShootSlot,
          companyName: companyName.trim(),
          companyRole,
          projectType,
          dateOfBirth: planId !== 'clients' ? dateOfBirth : '',
          isMinor: planId !== 'clients' && isMinor,
          visibilityScope:
            planId !== 'clients' && isMinor ? 'verified_clients_only' : 'public',
          guardianName: planId !== 'clients' && isMinor ? guardianName.trim() : '',
          guardianRelationship: planId !== 'clients' && isMinor ? guardianRelationship : '',
          guardianContact: planId !== 'clients' && isMinor ? guardianContact.trim() : '',
          guardianConsent: planId !== 'clients' && isMinor && guardianConsent,
        }),
      });
      const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
        saved?: boolean;
        onboarding?: OnboardingRecordPayload;
        notification?: { sent: boolean; channel: string; message: string };
        error?: string;
      };

      if (!res.ok || !data.saved || !data.onboarding) {
        setFormError(data.error || 'Unable to save onboarding profile. Please review your details.');
        return;
      }

      setSavedRecord(data.onboarding);
      setNotificationMessage(
        data.notification?.message ||
          `Confirmation notice dispatched to ${data.onboarding.email} and logged with NYC Model Management.`
      );
    } catch {
      setFormError('Network error saving onboarding profile.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const activeTierMeta =
    MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.find((t) => t.id === sessionInfo?.planId) || MEMBERSHIP_TIERS[0];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-white text-black flex flex-col">
      {/* Top Bar */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-xs border-b border-neutral-200">
        <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 h-14 flex items-center justify-between gap-8">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onNavigateHome()}
              className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-[-0.04em] text-black cursor-pointer"
            >
              nyc model club.
            </button>
            <span className="text-xs text-neutral-400">/</span>
            <span className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-600">
              Member Onboarding
            </span>
```
