```tsx
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onNavigateHome()}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:text-black whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>Back to Site</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="flex-1 max-w-[1060px] w-full mx-auto px-6 md:px-10 py-10 md:py-16">
        {verifying ? (
          <div className="border border-neutral-200 p-10 text-center">
            <p className="font-mono text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              Verifying Stripe Checkout Session
            </p>
            <h1 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl mt-2 text-black">
              {verifyingMessage}
            </h1>
          </div>
        ) : verifyError || !sessionInfo ? (
          <div className="border border-black p-8 md:p-12">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              Membership Activation Required
            </p>
            <h1 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl md:text-4xl mt-2 text-black">
              Complete Checkout to Begin Onboarding.
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-600 max-w-xl leading-relaxed">
              {verifyError ||
                'Select one of our three access tiers below to complete Stripe Checkout and unlock your personalized onboarding desk.'}
            </p>
            <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              {MEMBERSHIP_TIERS.map((tier) => (
                <button
                  key={tier.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onStartMembershipCheckout(tier.id)}
                  className="p-5 border border-neutral-300 hover:border-black text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500">
                    ${tier.monthlyPrice}/mo
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-display font-bold text-base text-black mt-1">{tier.name}</p>
                  <span className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold underline underline-offset-4">
                    <span>Activate Tier</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onNavigateHome()}
              className="mt-8 px-5 py-2.5 border border-black text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Return to NYC Model Club
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-10">
            {/* 1. Payment Confirmation Banner */}
            <section
              aria-label="Payment Confirmation"
              className="bg-[#0A0A0A] text-white border border-black p-7 md:p-10"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-start justify-between gap-6 pb-7 border-b border-neutral-800">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-neutral-400">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-white" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>Stripe Payment Confirmed · Instant Activation</span>
                  </div>
                  <h1
                    className="font-editorial italic font-normal text-white mt-3 leading-[1.04]"
                    style={{ fontSize: 'clamp(2.1rem, 4vw, 3.3rem)' }}
                  >
                    Welcome to NYC Model Club.
                  </h1>
                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-neutral-300 max-w-2xl leading-relaxed">
                    Your <strong className="text-white">{activeTierMeta.name}</strong> pass is now
                    active. Complete your onboarding profile below so our New York City Model
                    Management desk can finalize your access and roster visibility.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="border border-neutral-700 bg-black p-4 min-w-[220px] shrink-0">
                  <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
                    Verified Club Pass
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-mono font-bold text-lg text-white mt-1 tabular-nums">
                    {sessionInfo.memberCode}
                  </p>
                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-300 mt-1">
                    {sessionInfo.paymentStatus}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="pt-6 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6 text-xs">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 text-[10px]">
                    Active Membership Tier
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-white font-semibold mt-1">{activeTierMeta.name}</p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 text-[10px]">
                    Billing &amp; Status
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-white font-semibold mt-1 tabular-nums">
                    ${(sessionInfo.amountCents / 100).toFixed(2)} ·{' '}
                    {sessionInfo.billingCycle === 'annual' ? 'Annual Plan' : 'Monthly Plan'} · Paid
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 text-[10px]">
                    Account Email
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-white font-semibold mt-1 truncate">
                    {email || sessionInfo.email || 'Confirmed via Stripe'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* 2. Client & Agency Instant Access Deck ($99 Tier) */}
            {sessionInfo.planId === 'clients' && (
              <section
                aria-label="Client and Agency Instant Access"
                className="border border-black bg-neutral-50 p-7 md:p-8"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-4 pb-5 border-b border-neutral-200">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-neutral-500">
                      Clients &amp; Agencies Access · Unlocked Now
                    </p>
                    <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-black mt-1">
                      Instant Agency &amp; Casting Desk Access
                    </h2>
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                    15% Preferred Client Member Discount &amp; Private Rate Desk
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      onNavigateHome({ sectionId: 'management', clientUnlocked: true })
                    }
                    className="p-5 bg-black text-white text-left hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between gap-4"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-400">
                        01 · Direct Talent
                      </p>
                      <p className="font-display font-bold text-base mt-1">
                        Unlock Full Verified Roster
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300 mt-1.5">
                        Browse Runway, Editorial, Commercial &amp; AI Model Representation.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      <span>Open Roster</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      onNavigateHome({
                        sectionId: 'latest-work',
                        workCategory: 'Digitals',
                        clientUnlocked: true,
                      })
                    }
                    className="p-5 bg-white border border-black text-black text-left hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between gap-4"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
                        02 · Raw Casting Plates
                      </p>
                      <p className="font-display font-bold text-base mt-1">
                        View Unretouched Digitals
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1.5">
                        Inspect Polaroids, daylight measurements, and raw studio digitals.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      <span>Open Digitals</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </span>
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      onNavigateHome({ sectionId: 'media-production', clientUnlocked: true })
                    }
                    className="p-5 bg-white border border-black text-black text-left hover:bg-neutral-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between gap-4"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
                        03 · Booking &amp; Production
                      </p>
                      <p className="font-display font-bold text-base mt-1">
                        Open Agency Rate Desk
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1.5">
                        Book Models Only, Media Production Only, or Bundled Lookbook shoots.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      <span>Open Rate Desk</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </section>
            )}

            {/* 3. Confirmation Notification Banner after submission */}
            {savedRecord && (
              <section
                aria-label="Onboarding saved confirmation"
                className="border border-black bg-neutral-50 p-6 md:p-8"
                role="status"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-start justify-between gap-5">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-black">
                      <MailCheck className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                      <span>Onboarding Profile Saved &amp; Confirmation Triggered</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 className="font-display font-bold text-xl text-black">
                      Reference {savedRecord.id} · Received by New York City Model Management
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs md:text-sm text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl">
                      {notificationMessage ||
                        `A confirmation dispatch has been sent to ${savedRecord.email}. Our scouting and booking desk can now review your profile inside New Members & Onboarding.`}
                    </p>
                    {savedRecord.plan_id === 'studio' && savedRecord.studio_shoot_slot && (
                      <p className="text-xs font-mono text-black pt-1">
                        Requested SoHo / Crosby Street Studio Shoot: {savedRecord.studio_shoot_slot}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {(savedRecord.is_minor === 1 ||
                      savedRecord.profile_visibility === 'clients_only' ||
                      savedRecord.visibility_scope === 'verified_clients_only') && (
                      <p className="text-xs font-mono text-black pt-1">
                        Privacy Status: Private / Restricted (Minor Profile ·{' '}
                        {savedRecord.guardian_relationship || 'Guardian'}:{' '}
                        {savedRecord.guardian_name} · Visible exclusively to verified Clients &amp;
                        Agencies)
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onNavigateHome({ sectionId: 'management' })}
                      className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Explore NYC Model Club
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </section>
            )}

            {/* 4. Tier-Specific Onboarding Form */}
            <section
              aria-label="Tier onboarding form"
              className="border border-neutral-200 p-7 md:p-10 bg-white"
            >
              <div className="pb-6 border-b border-neutral-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-neutral-500">
                    {sessionInfo.planId === 'clients'
                      ? 'Client & Agency Company Onboarding'
                      : sessionInfo.planId === 'studio'
                        ? 'Model Profile & Studio Shoot Scheduling'
                        : 'Model & Talent Onboarding Profile Builder'}
                  </p>
                  <h2 className="font-editorial italic text-3xl text-black mt-1">
                    {sessionInfo.planId === 'clients'
                      ? 'Register Your Brand or Casting Desk.'
                      : 'Build Your Agency Scouting Profile.'}
                  </h2>
                </div>
                {savedRecord && (
                  <span className="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-600">
                    Saved · You may update below anytime
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleSubmitOnboarding} className="mt-7 space-y-6">
                {sessionInfo.planId === 'clients' ? (
                  <>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-company-name"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Company / Brand Name *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-company-name"
                          type="text"
                          required
                          value={companyName}
                          onChange={(e) => setCompanyName(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="e.g., Atelier Mercer / Vogue Casting"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-client-role"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Role *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          id="ob-client-role"
                          value={companyRole}
                          onChange={(e) => setCompanyRole(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        >
                          {CLIENT_ROLES.map((r) => (
                            <option key={r} value={r}>
                              {r}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-project-type"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Project Type *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          id="ob-project-type"
                          value={projectType}
                          onChange={(e) => setProjectType(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        >
                          {CLIENT_PROJECT_TYPES.map((pt) => (
                            <option key={pt} value={pt}>
                              {pt}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-client-email"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Confirmation Email *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-client-email"
                          type="email"
                          required
                          value={email}
                          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="casting@brand.com"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-client-contact"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Primary Contact Name
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-client-contact"
                          type="text"
                          value={fullName}
                          onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Jordan Sinclair"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-client-location"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Office / Shoot City
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-client-location"
                          type="text"
                          value={location}
                          onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="SoHo, New York"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-full-name"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Full Name *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-full-name"
                          type="text"
                          required
                          value={fullName}
                          onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Legal / Full Name"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-stage-name"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Stage Name
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-stage-name"
                          type="text"
                          value={stageName}
                          onChange={(e) => setStageName(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Optional working / editorial name"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-measurements"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Height / Measurements *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-measurements"
                          type="text"
                          required
                          value={measurements}
                          onChange={(e) => setMeasurements(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder={`e.g., 5'10" · 32-24-34 · Shoe 8.5`}
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-location"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Location *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-location"
                          type="text"
                          required
                          value={location}
                          onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="e.g., Manhattan / Brooklyn, NY"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-portfolio"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Instagram / Portfolio Link
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-portfolio"
                          type="text"
                          value={portfolioUrl}
                          onChange={(e) => setPortfolioUrl(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="@handle or https://portfolio.com"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-talent-email"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Confirmation Email *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-talent-email"
                          type="email"
                          required
                          value={email}
                          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="model@nycmodelclub.com"
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label
                          htmlFor="ob-dob"
                          className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                        >
                          Date of Birth *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="ob-dob"
                          type="date"
                          required
                          max={todayIso}
                          value={dateOfBirth}
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            const nextDob = e.target.value;
                            setDateOfBirth(nextDob);
                            const nextAge = calculateExactAge(nextDob);
                            if (nextAge === null || nextAge >= 18) {
                              setGuardianConsent(false);
                            }
                          }}
                          className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                        />
                        {calculatedAge !== null && (
                          <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-700">
                            {isMinor
                              ? `Age ${calculatedAge} · Minor Detected · Visibility: Verified Clients & Agencies Only`
                              : `Age ${calculatedAge} · Standard Roster Eligibility`}
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                      Under-18 Compliance: Applicants under 18 are automatically flagged as minors,
                      must be registered by a parent or legal guardian/tutor, and are hidden from
                      all open public Roster and Member Directory pages.
                    </p>

                    {isMinor && (
                      <div className="border border-black bg-neutral-50 p-5 space-y-4">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-black">
                            Parent / Legal Guardian Registration Required (Age Under 18)
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                            This profile is automatically set to Verified Clients &amp; Agencies
                            visibility only and will never appear on public Roster or Member
                            Directory pages.
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-guardian-name"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Parent / Legal Guardian Full Name *
                            </label>
                            <input
                              id="ob-guardian-name"
                              type="text"
                              required
                              value={guardianName}
                              onChange={(e) => setGuardianName(e.target.value)}
                              placeholder="Parent or legal guardian full name"
                              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                            />
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-guardian-relationship"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Relationship *
                            </label>
                            <select
                              id="ob-guardian-relationship"
                              required
                              value={guardianRelationship}
                              onChange={(e) => setGuardianRelationship(e.target.value)}
                              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                            >
                              <option value="">Select relationship...</option>
                              <option value="Parent">Parent</option>
                              <option value="Legal Guardian">Legal Guardian</option>
                              <option value="Legal Tutor">Legal Tutor</option>
                            </select>
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-guardian-contact"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Guardian Contact (Email / Phone) *
                            </label>
                            <input
                              id="ob-guardian-contact"
                              type="text"
                              required
                              value={guardianContact}
                              onChange={(e) => setGuardianContact(e.target.value)}
                              placeholder="guardian@domain.com or (212) 555-0194"
                              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer select-none">
                          <input
                            id="ob-guardian-consent"
                            type="checkbox"
                            required
                            checked={guardianConsent}
                            onChange={(e) => setGuardianConsent(e.target.checked)}
                            className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 accent-black shrink-0"
                          />
                          <span className="text-xs text-neutral-700 leading-snug">
                            I confirm I am the parent or legal guardian/tutor registering this minor
                            and approve private visibility restricted exclusively to verified
                            Clients &amp; Agencies. *
                          </span>
                        </label>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Digitals / Headshot Upload */}
                    <div className="border border-neutral-200 p-5 bg-neutral-50">
                      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-black">
                            Digitals / Headshot Upload
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
                            Upload a clean, unretouched daylight headshot or digital (JPG, PNG, WebP)
                            or paste a hosted portfolio photo URL.
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                          <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>{uploadBusy ? 'Optimizing...' : 'Upload Headshot'}</span>
                          <input
                            type="file"
                            accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                            onChange={(e) => void handleDigitalsUpload(e)}
                            className="sr-only"
                          />
                        </label>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-center">
                        <div className="md:col-span-9">
                          <input
                            type="url"
                            value={digitalsUrl.startsWith('data:image/') ? '' : digitalsUrl}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                              setDigitalsFileName('');
                              setDigitalsUrl(e.target.value);
                            }}
                            placeholder={
                              digitalsUrl.startsWith('data:image/')
                                ? `Uploaded file ready (${digitalsFileName || 'headshot.jpg'})`
                                : 'Or paste direct image URL (https://...)'
                            }
                            aria-label="Digitals image URL"
                            className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-xs focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                          />
                        </div>
                        <div className="md:col-span-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                          {digitalsUrl ? (
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <img
                                src={digitalsUrl}
                                alt="Uploaded digital headshot preview"
                                className="w-14 h-18 object-cover border border-neutral-300 bg-white"
                              />
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                  setDigitalsUrl('');
                                  setDigitalsFileName('');
                                }}
                                className="text-[11px] underline text-neutral-600 hover:text-black cursor-pointer"
                              >
                                Remove
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-neutral-500">
                              No photo attached
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* $59 Studio Access Exclusive: SoHo / Crosby Street Studio Shoot Scheduler */}
                    {sessionInfo.planId === 'studio' && (
                      <div className="border border-black p-6 bg-neutral-50">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest text-black">
                          <Camera className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
                          <span>$59 Studio Access Benefit · SoHo / Crosby Street</span>
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-black mt-1.5">
                          Request Your Monthly Studio Shoot Session
                        </h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
                          Select your preferred date and session window for our monthly NYC test
                          shoot, Polaroid update, and studio digitals at Crosby Street.
                        </p>

                        <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-studio-date"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Preferred Shoot Date *
                            </label>
                            <div className="relative">
                              <input
                                id="ob-studio-date"
                                type="date"
                                required
                                value={studioDate}
                                onChange={(e) => setStudioDate(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                              />
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-studio-window"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Preferred Time Window *
                            </label>
                            <select
                              id="ob-studio-window"
                              value={studioWindow}
                              onChange={(e) => setStudioWindow(e.target.value)}
                              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                            >
                              {STUDIO_TIME_WINDOWS.map((slot) => (
                                <option key={slot} value={slot}>
                                  {slot}
                                </option>
                              ))}
                            </select>
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <label
                              htmlFor="ob-studio-notes"
                              className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-700 mb-1.5"
                            >
                              Look / Shoot Focus
                            </label>
                            <input
                              id="ob-studio-notes"
                              type="text"
                              value={studioNotes}
                              onChange={(e) => setStudioNotes(e.target.value)}
                              placeholder="Digitals + Clean Beauty Test"
                              className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-white border border-neutral-300 text-sm focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                            />
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </>
                )}

                {formError && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
                    {formError}
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="pt-2 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-neutral-200">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-black shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>
                      Submitted directly to New York City Model Management · Need assistance?{' '}
                      <a
                        href={`mailto:${CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}`}
                        className="text-black underline underline-offset-4"
                      >
                        {CONTACT_CONFIG.directInquiriesEmail}
                      </a>
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submitting || uploadBusy}
                    className="px-7 py-3 bg-black text-white text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>
                      {submitting
                        ? 'Saving & Dispatching Confirmation...'
                        : savedRecord
                          ? 'Update Onboarding Profile'
                          : 'Complete Onboarding & Notify Agency'}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};

```

# src\components\StripeCheckoutModal.tsx

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { X, Lock, CreditCard, CheckCircle2, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { MembershipTier, ROLE_OPTIONS, calculateExactAge } from '../data/clubData';
import type { PaymentProvider } from './MembershipSection';

interface StripeCheckoutModalProps {
  tier: MembershipTier | null;
  billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
  paymentProvider: PaymentProvider;
  onChangePaymentProvider: (provider: PaymentProvider) => void;
  sessionId: string | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: (result: {
    planName: string;
    memberCode: string;
    planId: MembershipTier['id'];
    billingCycle: 'monthly' | 'annual';
    sessionId: string;
    email: string;
    fullName: string;
  }) => void;
}

const CHECKOUT_ROLES = [
  'Brand / Client',
  ...ROLE_OPTIONS.map((r) => r.label),
];

export const StripeCheckoutModal: React.FC<StripeCheckoutModalProps> = ({
  tier,
  billingCycle,
  paymentProvider,
  onChangePaymentProvider,
  sessionId,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
}) => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState(tier?.defaultRole || 'Model');
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState('');
  const [guardianName, setGuardianName] = useState('');
  const [guardianRelationship, setGuardianRelationship] = useState('');
  const [guardianContact, setGuardianContact] = useState('');
  const [guardianConsent, setGuardianConsent] = useState(false);
  const [submittedAsMinor, setSubmittedAsMinor] = useState(false);
  const [cardNumber, setCardNumber] = useState('');
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState('');
  const [cvc, setCvc] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirmedCode, setConfirmedCode] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tier) {
      setRole(tier.defaultRole || 'Model');
      setConfirmedCode(null);
      setErrorMsg(null);
      setSubmittedAsMinor(false);
    }
  }, [tier, sessionId]);

  if (!tier) return null;

  const isTalentTier = tier.id !== 'clients';
  const calculatedAge = isTalentTier ? calculateExactAge(dateOfBirth) : null;
  const isMinor = isTalentTier && calculatedAge !== null && calculatedAge < 18;
  const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  const providerLabel = paymentProvider === 'square' ? 'Square' : 'Stripe';
  const monthlyEquivalent =
    billingCycle === 'annual' ? tier.annualMonthlyPrice : tier.monthlyPrice;
  const totalDue = billingCycle === 'annual' ? tier.annualMonthlyPrice * 12 : tier.monthlyPrice;

  const formatCardNumber = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 16);
    return digits.replace(/(\d{4})(?=\d)/g, '$1 ');
  };

  const formatExpiry = (val: string) => {
    const digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4);
    if (digits.length >= 3) {
      return `${digits.slice(0, 2)}/${digits.slice(2)}`;
    }
    return digits;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg(null);

    if (!email.trim() || !email.includes('@')) {
      setErrorMsg('Please enter a valid applicant email address.');
      return;
    }
    if (!fullName.trim()) {
      setErrorMsg('Please enter your full name.');
      return;
    }
    if (isTalentTier) {
      if (calculatedAge === null) {
        setErrorMsg('Please enter a valid Date of Birth.');
        return;
      }
      if (isMinor) {
        if (guardianName.trim().length < 2) {
          setErrorMsg(
            'Applicants under 18 (minors) must be registered by a parent or legal guardian/tutor. Please enter their full name.'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (!guardianRelationship) {
          setErrorMsg(
            'Please select the guardian relationship (Parent, Legal Guardian, or Legal Tutor).'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (guardianContact.trim().length < 4) {
          setErrorMsg(
            'Please enter parent or legal guardian/tutor contact details (email or phone).'
          );
          return;
        }
        if (!guardianConsent) {
          setErrorMsg(
            'Please confirm parent or legal guardian/tutor registration and verified-client-only visibility.'
          );
          return;
        }
      }
    }
    const cleanCard = cardNumber.replace(/\s/g, '');
    if (cleanCard.length < 12) {
      setErrorMsg('Please enter a valid 16-digit card number.');
      return;
    }
    if (expiry.replace(/\D/g, '').length < 4 || cvc.length < 3) {
      setErrorMsg('Please complete the card expiration date and CVC.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/confirm-membership', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          email: email.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          role,
          planId: tier.id,
          billingCycle,
          paymentProvider,
          cardLast4: cleanCard.slice(-4),
          sessionId,
          dateOfBirth: isTalentTier ? dateOfBirth : '',
          isMinor,
          visibilityScope: isMinor ? 'verified_clients_only' : 'public',
          guardianName: isMinor ? guardianName.trim() : '',
          guardianRelationship: isMinor ? guardianRelationship : '',
          guardianContact: isMinor ? guardianContact.trim() : '',
          guardianConsent: isMinor && guardianConsent,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setErrorMsg(data.error || 'Payment authorization failed.');
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      setSubmittedAsMinor(Boolean(data.isMinor ?? isMinor));
      setConfirmedCode(data.memberCode);
      onSuccess({
        planName: tier.name,
        memberCode: data.memberCode,
        planId: tier.id,
        billingCycle,
        sessionId: data.sessionId || sessionId || '',
        email: email.trim(),
        fullName: fullName.trim(),
      });
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg(`Network error connecting to ${providerLabel} payment endpoint.`);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="stripe-checkout-title"
    >
      <div className="bg-white text-black w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto border border-black shadow-2xl">
        <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-black text-white flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Lock className="w-4 h-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="text-xs font-mono tracking-wider uppercase">
              NYC Model Club · {providerLabel} Application &amp; Fee Checkout
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close membership checkout"
            className="p-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {confirmedCode ? (
          <div className="p-8 md:p-10 text-center">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-black mb-4" />
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-neutral-500">
              Application Accepted &amp; Membership Active
            </p>
            <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-3xl mt-1">
              Welcome to NYC Model Club, {fullName}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 mt-3 max-w-md mx-auto">
              Your {tier.name} fee has been processed via {providerLabel} for{' '}
              <strong>{email}</strong> ({role}). Your verified Club Pass is{' '}
              <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-black">{confirmedCode}</span>.
              {submittedAsMinor &&
                ' This minor profile is marked private and visible exclusively to verified Clients & Agencies.'}
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="mt-8 px-8 py-3 bg-black text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors"
            >
              Enter NYC Model Club
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-5">
            <div className="md:col-span-2 bg-neutral-100 p-6 border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-neutral-200 flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="font-display font-extrabold text-sm tracking-tight text-black">
                  nyc model club.
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-4 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {billingCycle === 'annual' ? 'Annual Plan' : 'Monthly Fee'}
                </p>
                <h3 id="stripe-checkout-title" className="font-display font-bold text-xl mt-1">
                  {tier.name}
                </h3>

                <div className="mt-4 flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                  <span className="font-display font-bold text-3xl tabular-nums">
                    ${totalDue}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-neutral-600">
                    /{billingCycle === 'annual' ? 'year' : 'month'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {billingCycle === 'annual' && (
                  <p className="text-xs font-mono text-neutral-500 mt-1 tabular-nums">
                    Equivalent to ${monthlyEquivalent}/mo
                  </p>
                )}

                <ul className="mt-6 space-y-2 text-xs text-neutral-700 border-t border-neutral-200 pt-4">
                  {tier.features.slice(0, 4).map((f) => (
                    <li key={f}>· {f}</li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 font-mono">
                {sessionId ? `Session: ${sessionId}` : `TLS 1.3 · Powered by ${providerLabel}`}
              </div>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="md:col-span-3 p-6 space-y-3.5">
              {/* Payment Provider Switcher */}
              <div>
                <span className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Payment Method
                </span>
                <div
                  className="grid grid-cols-2 border border-neutral-300 bg-neutral-100 p-0.5"
                  role="group"
                  aria-label="Select payment provider"
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={paymentProvider === 'stripe'}
                    onClick={() => onChangePaymentProvider('stripe')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      paymentProvider === 'stripe'
                        ? 'bg-black text-white'
                        : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Pay with Stripe
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={paymentProvider === 'square'}
                    onClick={() => onChangePaymentProvider('square')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      paymentProvider === 'square'
                        ? 'bg-black text-white'
                        : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-black'
                    }`}
                  >
                    Pay with Square
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Full Name
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Jordan Sinclair"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Access Role
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={role}
                    onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    {CHECKOUT_ROLES.map((label) => (
                      <option key={label} value={label}>
                        {label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className={isTalentTier ? 'grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3' : ''}>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Account Email
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="applicant@nycmodelclub.com"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                {isTalentTier && (
                  <div>
                    <label
                      htmlFor="checkout-dob"
                      className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1"
                    >
                      Date of Birth *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="checkout-dob"
                      type="date"
                      required
                      max={todayIso}
                      value={dateOfBirth}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const nextDob = e.target.value;
                        setDateOfBirth(nextDob);
                        const nextAge = calculateExactAge(nextDob);
                        if (nextAge === null || nextAge >= 18) {
                          setGuardianConsent(false);
                        }
                      }}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-neutral-300 bg-white focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {isTalentTier && calculatedAge !== null && (
                <p className="font-mono text-[11px] text-neutral-700">
                  {isMinor
                    ? `Calculated Age: ${calculatedAge} · Minor Detected · Visibility: Verified Clients Only`
                    : `Calculated Age: ${calculatedAge} · Standard Membership Eligibility`}
                </p>
              )}

              {isMinor && (
                <div className="border border-black bg-neutral-50 p-3.5 space-y-2.5">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-black leading-snug">
                    Parent / Legal Guardian Registration Required (Under 18)
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                    Under-18 profiles are hidden from public pages and visible exclusively to
                    verified Clients &amp; Agencies.
                  </p>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Parent / Legal Guardian Full Name *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={guardianName}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Parent or legal guardian name"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                        Relationship *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        required
                        value={guardianRelationship}
                        onChange={(e) => setGuardianRelationship(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                      >
                        <option value="">Select relationship...</option>
                        <option value="Parent">Parent</option>
                        <option value="Legal Guardian">Legal Guardian</option>
                        <option value="Legal Tutor">Legal Tutor</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                      Parent / Guardian Contact (Email or Phone) *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={guardianContact}
                      onChange={(e) => setGuardianContact(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="guardian@domain.com or (212) 555-0194"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-white text-xs border border-neutral-300 focus:border-black focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <label className="flex items-start gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none pt-0.5">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      required
                      checked={guardianConsent}
                      onChange={(e) => setGuardianConsent(e.target.checked)}
                      className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 accent-black shrink-0"
                    />
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-700 leading-snug">
                      I confirm I am the parent or legal guardian/tutor registering this minor and
                      approve private visibility restricted to verified Clients &amp; Agencies. *
                    </span>
                  </label>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Card Details ({providerLabel})
                </label>
                <div className="border border-neutral-300 focus-within:border-black">
                  <div className="flex items-center px-3.5 py-2.5 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    <CreditCard className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 mr-2.5 shrink-0" />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      value={cardNumber}
                      onChange={(e) => setCardNumber(formatCardNumber(e.target.value))}
                      placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456"
                      className="w-full text-sm font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 divide-x divide-neutral-200">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      value={expiry}
                      onChange={(e) => setExpiry(formatExpiry(e.target.value))}
                      placeholder="MM/YY"
                      aria-label="Expiration date"
                      className="px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      maxLength={4}
                      value={cvc}
                      onChange={(e) => setCvc(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 4))}
                      placeholder="CVC"
                      aria-label="Security code"
                      className="px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-mono tabular-nums focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {errorMsg && (
                <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium" role="alert">
                  {errorMsg}
                </p>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="w-full py-3 bg-black text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                {submitting
                  ? `Authorizing with ${providerLabel}...`
                  : `Apply & Pay $${totalDue} via ${providerLabel}`}
              </button>

              <p className="flex items-start justify-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 text-center leading-relaxed">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>
                  Open to all models · Minors under 18 must be registered by a parent or guardian and
                  remain hidden from public pages, visible only to verified clients
                </span>
              </p>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

```

```ts
  visibilityScope?: VisibilityScope;
}

export function calculateExactAge(dateOfBirth: string): number | null {
  const match = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(dateOfBirth.trim());
  if (!match) return null;
  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = Number(match[3]);
  const parsed = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  if (
    parsed.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
    parsed.getUTCMonth() + 1 !== month ||
    parsed.getUTCDate() !== day
  ) {
    return null;
  }
  const now = new Date();
  let age = now.getFullYear() - year;
  const monthDiff = now.getMonth() + 1 - month;
  if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && now.getDate() < day)) {
    age -= 1;
  }
  if (age < 0 || age > 120) return null;
  return age;
}
```

# src\index.css

```css
@import "tailwindcss";

@theme {
  --font-display: "Plus Jakarta Sans", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
  --font-sans: "Plus Jakarta Sans", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
  --font-serif: "Cormorant Garamond", Georgia, serif;
  --font-mono: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, monospace;
}

@layer base {
  html {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    color: #000000;
    background-color: #ffffff;
  }

  h1, h2, h3, .font-display {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
  }
}

.font-editorial {
  font-family: var(--font-serif);
}

/* Crisp tabular figures for measurements, commission math, and Stripe inputs */
.tabular-nums {
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

/* Subtle film grain overlay for monochrome editorial plates */
.film-grain {
  background-image: radial-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) 1px, transparent 0),
    radial-gradient(rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08) 1px, transparent 0);
  background-size: 3px 3px;
  background-position: 0 0, 1.5px 1.5px;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }
}

```

# STRIPE_ENV.md

```md
# Stripe Configuration & Key Swap Guide

NYC Model Club reads all Stripe credentials exclusively from environment variables (`process.env`). Never hardcode live Stripe keys in source files.

## Modes Supported

1. **Test-Mode Fallback (Default when `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` is unset)**
   - Membership (`$29` / `$59` / `$99`), Model Bookings, Media & Visual Works Bookings, and `/onboarding` work immediately without throwing `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` missing errors.
   - To disable fallback in strict production once keys are installed, set `STRIPE_TEST_MODE="false"`.

2. **Stripe Test Mode (`sk_test_...`)**
   - Uses Stripe's official hosted Checkout (`checkout.stripe.com`) in test mode.
   - Test cards (`4242 4242 4242 4242`) can be used on Stripe Checkout.

3. **Stripe Live Production (`sk_live_...` + `whsec_...`)**
   - Automatically activates live hosted Checkout and strict `checkout.session.completed` webhook verification (`POST /api/webhook`).
   - Test/fallback session IDs (`cs_dev_*`) and `/api/confirm-membership` are automatically disabled (`403`).

---

## 1. `.env` File (Local or VPS)

\`\`\`env
APP_URL="https://yourdomain.com"
STRIPE_SECRET_KEY="sk_test_your_test_secret_key_here"
STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY="pk_test_your_test_publishable_key_here"
VITE_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY="pk_test_your_test_publishable_key_here"
STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET="whsec_your_webhook_signing_secret_here"
STRIPE_TEST_MODE="true"
\`\`\`

When switching to live production, update `sk_test_...` -> `sk_live_...`, `pk_test_...` -> `pk_live_...`, update `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` to your live endpoint's `whsec_...`, set `STRIPE_TEST_MODE="false"`, rebuild (`npm run build`), and restart the server.

---

## 2. PM2 (`ecosystem.config.cjs`)

\`\`\`js
module.exports = {
  apps: [
    {
      name: 'nyc-model-club',
      script: 'server.ts',
      interpreter: 'node',
      env: {
        NODE_ENV: 'production',
        PORT: 3000,
        APP_URL: 'https://yourdomain.com',
        STRIPE_SECRET_KEY: 'sk_test_your_test_secret_key_here',
        STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY: 'pk_test_your_test_publishable_key_here',
        STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET: 'whsec_your_webhook_signing_secret_here',
        STRIPE_TEST_MODE: 'true',
      },
      env_production: {
        NODE_ENV: 'production',
        PORT: 3000,
        APP_URL: 'https://yourdomain.com',
        STRIPE_SECRET_KEY: 'sk_live_your_live_secret_key_here',
        STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY: 'pk_live_your_live_publishable_key_here',
        STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET: 'whsec_your_live_webhook_secret_here',
        STRIPE_TEST_MODE: 'false',
      },
    },
  ],
};
\`\`\`

Restart after updating keys:

\`\`\`bash
pm2 restart nyc-model-club --update-env
\`\`\`

---

## 3. systemd (`/etc/systemd/system/nyc-model-club.service`)

\`\`\`ini
[Service]
WorkingDirectory=/var/www/nyc-model-club
ExecStart=/usr/bin/node server.ts
Restart=always
Environment=NODE_ENV=production
Environment=PORT=3000
Environment=APP_URL=https://yourdomain.com
Environment=STRIPE_SECRET_KEY=sk_test_your_test_secret_key_here
Environment=STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=pk_test_your_test_publishable_key_here
Environment=STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET=whsec_your_webhook_signing_secret_here
Environment=STRIPE_TEST_MODE=true
\`\`\`

Reload and restart after swapping to `sk_live_...`:

\`\`\`bash
sudo systemctl daemon-reload
sudo systemctl restart nyc-model-club
\`\`\`

```

# vite.config.ts

```ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      // Copies src/assets into dist/ so root-relative URLs like
      // "/src/assets/images/..." (e.g. generate_image output) still resolve
      // in production builds. Do not remove.
      {
        name: 'copy-unbundled-src-assets',
        apply: 'build',
        closeBundle(error?: Error): void {
          if (error) {
            return;
          }
          const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
          const srcAssets = path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets');
          const distAssets = path.resolve(distDir, 'src/assets');
          if (fs.existsSync(distDir) && fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
            fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distAssets, {recursive: true, force: true});
          }
        },
      },
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

```
